Conclusion: μ²-Bench 为多语言机器遗忘提供了更系统的评测框架，能够检验目标知识在不同语言、训练/未见语言及跨语言表达下是否被真正消除。研究说明，面向多语言特性设计和评估遗忘方法，是实现可靠 MMU 的必要条件。

Abstract: Undesired information such as harmful content and private data propagates through Multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) via direct training and indirect cross-linguistic spread. Multilingual Machine Unlearning (MMU) aims to remove such information, yet its evaluation remains underexplored, leaving unclear whether unlearning truly eliminates target knowledge across all languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce $μ^2$-Bench, an MMU benchmark that simulates the full pipeline of memorization, unlearning, and evaluation across diverse languages. It 1) spans a broad set of languages, 2) evaluates on both training and hold-out languages, and 3) assesses knowledge as dispersed across multiple languages. We show that successful MMU requires methods that reflect multilingual characteristics, and conduct analysis to provide deeper insights into MMU.

</details>


### [100] [Aligning with Lived Experience: Heterogeneous Benefits of Fine Tuning in Mental Health Support Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21075)
*Mohit Chandra,Nabin Kim,Eli Min,Aamogh Sawant,Tanmay Sutar,Munmun De Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出COPES社区中心化同伴支持数据集及三轴评估框架，用于评估大语言模型生成的心理健康同伴支持是否符合社区经验与需求。结果显示，基于COPES进行SFT和DPO后，模型在策略对齐、情绪与语气对齐方面明显提升，但效果因子社区和应对策略而异，并出现偏向问题聚焦建议、抑制情绪聚焦策略的分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业心理健康服务获取受限，许多人转向Reddit等在线社区寻求基于真实生活经验的同伴支持，但大量求助内容得不到回应。现有研究更关注LLM的临床基准表现，对其能否生成符合社区观点、具有生活经验导向且适配心理健康求助场景的支持性回复研究不足。

Method: 构建COPES（COmmunity-centered Peer Engaged Support）数据集，并提出包含三个维度的评估框架，用于衡量模型回复与社区视角的对齐程度。对通用模型及其零样本、监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）版本进行比较，重点分析策略对齐、情绪与语气对齐，以及不同子社区和应对策略之间的差异。

Result: 在COPES上进行后训练显著提升了模型的策略对齐能力，通用模型的提升超过50%，同时改善了情绪与语气对齐。然而，提升并不均衡：不同subreddit社区及用户请求的应对策略之间存在显著差异。后训练还造成分布偏移，使模型更倾向于提供问题聚焦型建议，并减少情绪聚焦型策略。

Conclusion: 社区驱动数据能够有效改善LLM心理健康同伴支持回复的对齐性，但单纯后训练无法保证跨社区、跨需求的一致表现。未来需要关注子社区差异、策略分布平衡，以及防止模型过度偏向问题解决而忽视情绪支持。

Abstract: As access to professional mental healthcare remains limited, many individuals turn to online platforms such as Reddit to seek peer support situated within human lived experience. However, a significant portion of such queries go unanswered, presenting an opportunity for using Large Language Models (LLMs) to fill this gap. While LLMs have demonstrated strong performance on clinical benchmarks, their ability to generate lived-experience informed and community-aligned peer support is underexplored. Addressing this gap, we introduce the COmmunity-centered Peer Engaged Support (COPES) dataset and a three-axis evaluation framework to assess LLM alignment with community perspectives to mental health support seeking queries. Evaluating zero-shot and post-trained (SFT and DPO) models, we show that post-training on COPES significantly improves Strategy Alignment (>50% for general-purpose models) and alignment in Emotion & Tone. However, we also observe that such improvements are heterogeneous and alignment improvements vary significantly across subreddits and requested coping strategies. Furthermore, post-training induces distributional shifts, heavily favoring problem-focused recommendations while suppressing emotion-focused strategies. Together, this work shows that while curating community-driven data improves the alignment of LLM responses, model performance remains disparate across distinct sub-communities and specific mental health needs.

</details>


### [101] [Geometry of Values: Task Vector Composition for Ethical Preference Alignment in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21094)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个跨语言道德价值冲突基准，并通过微调与任务向量迁移分析模型的价值偏好、指令遵循偏差及其可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在涉及相互冲突的道德价值时，可能表现出隐性偏见、首选项偏差以及跨语言不一致；同时，模型的价值偏好究竟源于抽象价值表征，还是仅仅学习了数据中的表面相关性，尚未得到充分区分。因此，本文旨在构建系统化的跨语言评测数据集，并探索如何识别和操控特定价值偏好。

Method: 构建包含12,000个二选一道德困境实例的数据集，覆盖三类成对价值冲突：诚实与正义、正义与自主、以及自主与诚实，并提供印地语、阿拉伯语、西班牙语和中文翻译。使用GPT-5-mini和Llama-3.2-1/3B进行基准测试，比较无策略条件下的跨语言偏好及首选项偏差。随后对Llama模型进行普通监督微调和直接偏好优化（DPO）微调。为区分数据相关性与抽象价值方向，计算特定价值偏好的任务向量，并将其相对于通用指令遵循向量进行正交化；最后利用任务算术组合向量，以获得相反价值立场的模型。

Result: GPT-5-mini在五种语言中、未提供明确策略时，均稳定地偏向诚实而非自主。Llama-3.2-1/3B表现出明显的首选项偏差，即倾向选择题目中的第一个选项。普通微调和DPO微调都能有效消除该偏差，并将准确率提升至98%以上。任务向量正交化实验表明，该方法能够分离特定价值偏好的方向；经过任务算术后，可以生成具有相反价值立场的模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明，大语言模型的道德价值判断具有显著的跨语言稳定偏好和结构性输出偏差，但这些偏差可以通过微调有效缓解。相对于通用指令遵循能力，正交化任务向量有助于提取更具针对性的价值偏好方向，并支持对模型价值立场进行可控反转。不过，摘要尚未说明数据集标注质量、不同语言之间的文化等价性以及“准确率”的具体定义，这些因素仍是评估结论可靠性的重要限制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications that must weigh clashing moral values, yet even strong models exhibit hidden biases and brittle instruction-following across languages. We introduce a 12,000-instance dataset of two-option dilemmas covering pairwise three value conflicts: Honesty vs. Justice, Justice vs. Autonomy, and Autonomy vs. Honesty, along with their translations into Hindi, Arabic, Spanish, and Chinese, to probe cross-lingual behavior. Benchmarking on GPT-5-mini reveals that it consistently favors Honesty over Autonomy across all five languages when no policy is given. The Llama-3.2-1/3B models exhibit strong first-option bias; however, both plain fine-tuning and Direct Preference Optimization fine-tuning effectively remove this bias, increasing accuracy to greater than 98%. In order to decouple the effect of learning correlations in the dataset from abstract values, we propose a task vector transfer based experiment where after computing the task vectors for a direction of value preference we orthogonalize it with respect to the general instruction following vector. Our experiment shows that this method is effective in isolating the direction of the specific value preference that can successfully be used to conduct task arithmetic to obtain a model with the opposite stance.

</details>


### [102] [From Task Success to Productive Success: Evaluating Human-AI Collaboration by Quality and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21117)
*Saki Imai,Mert İnan,Malihe Alikhani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出以“结果质量/交互成本”衡量人机协作生产力，发现相同质量可能对应巨大成本差异，且不同任务的最优交互策略不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生产力评估主要关注完成时间、经济价值或结果质量，常把协作过程视为黑箱，忽略达到结果所需的交互成本，因此难以区分高效成功与高成本成功。论文借鉴经济学中的生产力思想，旨在建立同时考虑产出质量与交互投入的评估框架。

Method: 作者基于两个数据集、四类任务，构建以结果质量相对于交互成本为核心的人机协作生产力指标，并分析交互过程中的对话行为、用户主观评价、代理探询时机以及用户修复交互所付出的努力。研究进一步比较不同任务中的质量—成本关系及会话表现。

Result: 主要发现包括：1）质量评分相同的会话，其交互成本最高可相差70倍；2）质量—成本关系具有任务依赖性，一些任务受益于更长的交互，另一些任务更适合快速收敛；3）用户主观评分不能可靠替代生产力指标；4）高生产力会话通常表现为代理更早进行探询，且用户投入较少精力修复交互。

Conclusion: 将结果质量与交互成本结合，能够把传统评估中被隐藏的协作成本显性化，并区分“高效成功”和“高成本成功”。该框架表明，对话过程分析不仅有助于更准确地评价AI系统，也能指导系统交互策略和设计优化。

Abstract: AI productivity is often measured by task completion time, economic value, or improvements in outcome quality. However, these measures usually treat collaboration as a black box where they capture what output was produced, but not the interaction cost required to produce it. Motivated by economics literature, we introduce a productivity-oriented framework for evaluating human-AI collaboration as outcome quality relative to interaction cost. Across two datasets spanning four tasks, we show that: (1) sessions with identical quality ratings can differ by up to 70 times in interaction cost; (2) quality-cost relationships vary by task, with some tasks rewarding extended interaction and others favoring fast convergence; (3) subjective user ratings are not reliable substitutes for productivity; and (4) productive sessions are characterized by agents probing earlier and users spending less effort repairing the interaction. By distinguishing productive success from costly success, our framework makes interactional cost visible and shows how dialogue analysis can inform the evaluation and design of AI systems.

</details>


### [103] [Scaling Forced Alignment to End-User Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21145)
*Lawry Sorenson,Michael Crandall,Eric K. Ringger,Stephen D. Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向长音频—文本强制对齐的高效方法：结合 Hirschberg 线性空间对齐与基于受限随机游走的搜索剪枝，在保持对齐质量的同时显著降低内存和运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Viterbi 算法的强制对齐实现通常具有二次时间和空间复杂度，处理长时长音频时会产生极高的内存占用和计算成本，难以用于大规模在线数据挖掘。因此，论文旨在解决长序列对齐的可扩展性问题，并兼顾转录错误带来的不确定性。

Method: 方法包含两项优化。第一，采用 Hirschberg 算法以线性内存完成对齐，避免保存完整的动态规划矩阵。第二，将语音与文本的对齐过程建模为受约束的随机游走，并据此进行搜索空间剪枝；该剪枝机制允许根据任意置信度进行控制，同时考虑转录错误。

Result: 在三小时输入上，Hirschberg 优化将内存占用从 140 GB 降至 5 MB，并在 CPU 上生成与原方法完全一致的对齐结果，耗时约为 torchaudio 的三分之一。在超过 20 分钟的输入上，进一步的剪枝带来约 2 倍加速；在超过 98% 的测试案例中保持了对齐准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作有效缓解了长音频强制对齐中的内存和计算瓶颈。线性空间 Hirschberg 对齐显著提升了可扩展性，随机游走剪枝进一步提高了速度，并在大多数测试中保持准确性，适合长时长音频和大规模训练数据挖掘场景。

Abstract: The Viterbi algorithm has been previously used to perform forced alignment of audio to text to mine training data from online resources. However, many existing implementations have quadratic time and space complexity, scaling poorly to long input sequences. We propose two optimizations to address this issue. First, we apply the Hirschberg algorithm to perform the alignment in place using linear memory. Second, we model the alignment between speech and text as a constrained random walk, allowing us to prune the search space with arbitrary confidence while accounting for transcription errors. The Hirschberg optimization reduces memory usage from 140 GB to 5 MB for three-hour inputs while producing identical alignments in one-third the time of torchaudio when both run on a CPU. We achieve an additional 2x speedup with pruning on inputs longer than 20 minutes while preserving alignment accuracy in more than 98% of tested cases.

</details>


### [104] [CoLearn: An Agentic Tutor that Learns its Learner in a Human--AI Co-Learning Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21154)
*Kailai He,Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Runcong Zhao,Yulan He,Jiazheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoLearn 是一种面向个性化教学的智能辅导系统，通过持续更新的学习者状态记忆、软证据贝叶斯知识追踪和自适应出题，依据学习者的薄弱知识点与反复出现的误解生成下一道题；盲测显示个性化题目约有68%—69%的偏好率，模拟实验表明系统对学习者掌握度的估计能够逐渐接近真实状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数智能辅导工具依赖固定题库，并将答错简化为单一的二元信号，难以识别学习者具体掌握程度、错误原因及持续存在的误解，也无法有效决定下一步最有帮助的教学问题。因此，论文旨在构建能够持续积累证据、跟踪个体学习状态并动态调整教学内容的智能辅导系统。

Method: 论文提出 CoLearn 交互式智能辅导系统，包含三个核心部分：1）持久化学习者状态记忆，按主题维护掌握度，并采用软证据版本的贝叶斯知识追踪，由大语言模型充当连续观测函数；2）自适应题目生成，根据学习者最薄弱的主题和反复出现的误解生成后续问题；3）证据视图，通过实时学习进度可视化和盲法 A/B 比较，使个性化过程具有可见性和可测试性。整体形成“练习—收集证据—更新学习状态—生成下一题”的迭代教学闭环。

Result: 在盲法 A/B 评估中，基于学习者记忆生成的个性化题目获得了约68%—69%的偏好率，优于非个性化题目。在具有隐藏真实掌握度的学习者模拟实验中，系统对学习者掌握度的信念能够随着证据累积逐渐收敛到真实掌握度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将持续更新的学习者状态记忆与自适应出题结合，能够提升题目的个性化程度和用户偏好，并使系统更准确地估计学习者知识状态。CoLearn 为构建可解释、可验证、证据驱动的智能辅导系统提供了初步方案，但摘要未说明真实课堂中的长期学习增益、样本规模及与更强基线的比较，因此其实际教学效果仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Good tutoring adapts to the individual: it tracks what a learner knows, notices why they go wrong, and asks the next question that will help most. Most deployed tutoring tools instead serve fixed item banks and treat a wrong answer as a single bit of signal. We present CoLearn, an interactive, agentic tutor that supports an iterative tutoring loop: the learner practises, and the system builds an evidence-grounded memory of the learner's mastery and misconceptions. This memory is updated as evidence accumulates and is used to generate the next personalised question. CoLearn has three components: (i) a persistent learner-state memory that updates per-topic mastery with a soft-evidence variant of Bayesian Knowledge Tracing, where a large language model acts as a continuous observation function; (ii) adaptive question generation that targets the learner's weakest topic and recurring misconceptions; and (iii) an evidence view that makes personalisation visible and testable through live progress visualisation and blind A/B comparison. In blind A/B evaluation, questions conditioned on this memory are preferred over non-personalised ones 68-69% of the time, and in persona simulations with hidden ground-truth mastery the agent's belief converges toward the learner's true mastery.

</details>


### [105] [Not All Irregularity Is Equal: Causally Isolating a Rare Failure Mode in Japanese Morphological Inflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21179)
*Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究日语过去时动词屈折中的错误集中现象，发现总体准确率无法反映一个极低频、具有特定正字法—音系转换要求的异常子类所造成的大量残余错误。通过字符级Transformer、多随机种子评估及控制消融实验，论文证明错误主要源于低频模式与特定正字法过程的交互，而非“不规则性”本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经形态生成系统通常报告较高的总体准确率，但总体指标可能掩盖集中在稀有形态子类中的系统性错误。本文旨在细粒度诊断日语过去时动词屈折的错误来源，特别考察平假名是否承载了可用于学习形态音系结构的正字法信息，并区分“不规则性”与低频、特定正字法过程之间的作用。

Method: 研究采用两种字符级Transformer架构，在五个随机种子下进行评估。首先对日语过去时动词屈折进行按形态子类划分的正字法感知分析，比较各子类的数据占比与错误占比。随后开展控制消融实验，分别移除目标稀有子类和全部不规则动词，以进行因果隔离并比较准确率变化。

Result: 两种模型的总体准确率均超过97%。然而，词干以/e/结尾、在过去时后缀前需要发生促音化的稀有异常子类不足数据的1%，却占剩余错误的约30%—43%，其对总错误的贡献约为其数据普及率的34—48倍。移除这一单一子类带来的准确率提升，大于移除所有不规则动词所带来的提升。

Conclusion: 神经形态学习中的错误集中并非由不规则性这一宽泛属性单独造成，而是由极低频形态模式与特定正字法过程的交互驱动。论文建议形态学评估采用细粒度子类分析，并指出这些发现对数据高效及具有发展心理学合理性的语言模型预训练具有启示意义。

Abstract: Neural morphological generation systems often achieve high aggregate accuracy on benchmark datasets, yet such performance can conceal systematic errors clustered in rare morphological subclasses. We present an orthography-aware diagnosis of Japanese past-tense verb inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium but as a representational system that encodes morphophonological structure. Using two character-level Transformer architectures evaluated across five random seeds, we show that although both systems exceed 97% aggregate accuracy, a single structurally specific irregular subtype, verbs whose stems end in /e/ and require gemination before the past-tense suffix and make up fewer than 1% of the data, accounts for a disproportionate 30-43% share of residual errors and contributes roughly 34-48x its prevalence to total errors. We then move from diagnosis to causal isolation: controlled ablation experiments show that removing this subtype alone produces larger accuracy gains than removing all irregular verbs combined. These findings indicate that error concentration in neural morphological learning is not driven by irregularity per se, but by the interaction between extreme low-frequency morphological patterns and specific orthographic processes. We argue that morphological evaluation should incorporate fine-grained subclass analysis, and discuss implications for data-efficient, developmentally plausible language model pretraining.

</details>


### [106] [When Better Turns Do Not Make Better Agents: Diagnosing the Gap Between Next-Turn Metrics and Workflow Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21187)
*Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Xue-Yong Fu,Gundeep Singh,Karol Chang,Kevin Sanders,Shi Zong,Tania Habib,Julien Bouvier Tremblay,Shayna Gardiner,Harsh Saini,Matthias Lee,Elena Khasanova,Quinten McNamara,Shashi Bhushan TN*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究表明：在基于标准交互历史的逐轮下一步预测评估中取得的进步，并不能可靠预测模型在自主、多轮端到端工作流中的成功。监督微调（SFT）提升了文本轮次表现和金标准历史下的下一轮成功率，但未能稳定转化为完整工作流执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估常以金标准交互历史为条件，逐次判断模型下一步动作是否正确。这种评估可能掩盖模型在自主执行时因早期错误、状态偏移、工具调用失败和错误累积而产生的问题。研究旨在检验下一轮评估改进是否能够预测真实的端到端工作流能力，并进一步区分文本质量、局部动作正确性、工具执行和整体任务完成之间的关系。

Method: 研究比较了Qwen3（4B、14B）和Gemma 3（4B、12B）模型在监督微调（SFT）前后的表现，任务为多轮客户支持工作流。评估采用两类设置：一是基于金标准交互历史的逐轮下一步预测，二是模型自主推进的整体工作流评估。同时考察文本轮次成功率、总体下一轮成功率、工具相关指标以及严格轨迹完成率。

Result: SFT在所有模型上均稳定提升了文本轮次成功率；在金标准历史评估下，四个模型的总体下一轮成功率也全部提高。然而，这些提升没有迁移到自主工作流执行中。工具相关收益因指标和模型而异。整体工作流评估中，四个SFT模型均未实现可靠成功，严格轨迹完成率最高仅为10.4%。

Conclusion: 下一轮评估并不是端到端工作流成功的可靠代理指标。模型可能在理想历史条件下能够做出局部正确预测，却无法在自主执行中维持正确状态、连续调用工具并完成完整任务。因此，智能体评估应分别报告文本质量、局部动作正确性、工具执行能力和端到端任务完成率，避免用单一的逐轮指标替代真实工作流表现。

Abstract: Agent models are frequently evaluated one decision at a time, where the model predicts the next action based on the gold interaction history, which is scored against a reference. We investigate whether improvement under this protocol is predictive of improved autonomous workflow execution. We study pre-SFT and supervised fine-tuned (SFT) Qwen3 models at 4B and 14B parameters and Gemma 3 models at 4B and 12B parameters on multi-turn customer-support workflows. We find that SFT consistently improves text-turn success, and that overall next-turn success increases for every model under gold-history evaluation. However, these improvements do not transfer to autonomous workflow execution. Tool-specific gains also vary across metrics and models. None of the four SFT models succeeds under holistic workflow evaluation, with strict trajectory completion reaching at most 10.4% workflow success. Our results show that next-turn evaluation is not a reliable proxy for workflow success, motivating separate reporting of text quality, local action correctness, tool execution, and end-to-end task completion.

</details>


### [107] [Hallucination-R1: Robustness-Oriented Paraphrase Generation for Factual Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21227)
*Wenhan Yu,Wenxin Wu,Hao Wang,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HALLUCINATION-R1，一种面向鲁棒性的释义生成框架，用于生成语义等价但能够暴露问答模型事实不稳定性的改写问题。该方法通过两阶段优化，在保持语义忠实与多样性的同时，最大化下游模型在释义变化下的事实一致性退化。实验表明，该框架在多个数据集和模型家族上能够有效发现非表层、非语义漂移导致的事实鲁棒性缺陷，并生成可用于提升释义鲁棒准确率的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实幻觉研究通常关注模型直接输出错误事实，但较少考察模型在语义等价的不同表述下是否保持事实一致。作者关注一种更隐蔽的失稳现象：模型对原始问题回答正确，却在等价释义下回答错误。通用释义生成存在两类问题：接近复制的改写提供的鲁棒性信号弱，过度多样化的改写又可能破坏原始语义。因此，需要一种能够同时保证语义等价、多样性和挑战性的释义生成方法，以系统暴露模型的潜在事实不稳定性。

Method: 提出 HALLUCINATION-R1 框架，采用两阶段优化。第一阶段学习稳定生成语义保持且具有多样性的释义，避免语义漂移和机械复制。第二阶段基于下游事实问答模型的表现进行奖励优化，偏向生成能够诱发事实一致性下降、但仍与原问题语义等价的释义。该框架本质上将释义生成转化为面向鲁棒性评估的挑战样本构造问题，并通过生成数据进一步开展轻量微调实验。

Result: 在 SimpleQuestions、PopQA 和 TruthfulQA 上，HALLUCINATION-R1 在释义一致性与释义多样性之间取得较强平衡，并能在多个模型家族和数据集上发现鲁棒性缺陷。进一步分析表明，观察到的失败不能简单归因于表面形式变化或语义漂移，而体现了模型在保持语义不变时的非平凡事实不稳定性。轻量微调结果显示，使用该框架生成的数据可以提升模型在释义变化下的鲁棒准确率。摘要未提供具体数值、基线名称或显著性检验结果，因此对性能提升幅度无法进一步量化。

Conclusion: 本文说明，事实一致性不仅取决于模型能否回答原始问题，还取决于其能否在语义等价的表述变化下维持相同事实判断。HALLUCINATION-R1 能够有效生成语义忠实且具有攻击性的释义，用于揭示和缓解此类潜在失稳。研究结果支持将释义诱导的事实不一致视为一种独立且重要的鲁棒性问题，同时表明自动生成的挑战性释义数据具有评测和训练价值。

Abstract: Factual hallucination is commonly defined by incorrect factual outputs. We study a paraphrase-induced hallucination setting, where a model answers a factual question correctly in its original form but generates an incorrect answer under a semantically equivalent paraphrase. Such inconsistencies expose latent factual instability under semantic invariance. However, general-purpose paraphrases are often insufficient as robustness-oriented supervision: near-copy paraphrases provide weak signals, while overly diverse paraphrases may break semantic equivalence. In this paper, we propose HALLUCINATION-R1, a robustness-oriented paraphrase generation framework that learns to produce semantically faithful yet robustness-challenging paraphrases for factual consistency. Through two-stage optimization, it first stabilizes meaning-preserving and diverse paraphrasing, then rewards paraphrases that reveal factual consistency degradation in downstream QA models. Experiments on SimpleQuestions, PopQA, and TruthfulQA show that HALLUCINATION-R1 achieves a strong consistency--diversity trade-off and exposes robustness failures across multiple model families and datasets. Further analyses indicate that these failures are not reducible to surface-level artifacts or semantic drift, but reveal non-trivial factual instability under meaning-preserving variation. A lightweight fine-tuning study also shows that HALLUCINATION-R1-generated data improves robust accuracy under paraphrase variations, suggesting its utility for robustness-oriented training. Our code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/yuwenhan07/Hallucination-R1.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Reference-Based Evaluation: Reward Models for Meta-Evaluation of Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21231)
*Ruotian Wu,Bill E. Johnson,Gene Saunders,Osama Hamzeh,Ankit Vadehra,Pascal Poupart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RM-EVAL，一种基于人类偏好训练、无需参考答案的语法错误纠正（GEC）评价模型；同时将其作为奖励信号，通过RGTG在线引导冻结的基础模型生成更高质量的纠错结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEC评价指标（如M²和ERRANT）依赖参考答案，并隐含假设参考集合覆盖所有有效修改，因此可能惩罚那些语法正确、语义保持一致但表达方式不同的纠错结果。论文旨在构建更接近人类判断、无需黄金参考答案的评价方法，并进一步探索评价模型能否直接用于改进GEC生成。

Method: 使用SEEDA中的人类偏好数据训练奖励模型RM-EVAL，使其能够在完整序列和部分序列两个层面预测人类质量判断。随后提出Reward-Guided Text Generation（RGTG）：冻结基础GEC模型，在在线解码过程中利用RM-EVAL提供的奖励信号，引导生成过程选择更优候选。

Result: 在SEEDA数据集上，RM-EVAL与人类排序表现出较强一致性。将其用于RGTG后，GEC系统在奖励指标和外部验证指标上均获得持续提升。

Conclusion: RM-EVAL和RGTG构成了一个统一框架：前者实现不依赖参考答案的GEC质量评价，后者利用同一奖励模型改进纠错生成。结果表明，人类偏好奖励模型既可作为元评价器，也可作为有效的生成优化信号。

Abstract: Reference-based metrics for Grammatical Error Correction (GEC) such as M$^2$ and ERRANT assume that the reference set enumerates all valid edits, and therefore often penalize corrections that are grammatical and meaning-preserving but phrased differently. We introduce RM-EVAL, a reward model trained on human preference data from SEEDA, as a reference-free meta-evaluator that predicts human-like quality judgments at both full-sequence and partial-sequence levels. Beyond evaluation, we show that the same reward model can be used as a learning signal to improve GEC generation via Reward-Guided Text Generation (RGTG), which keeps a base GEC model frozen and performs online, reward-driven decoding. Across SEEDA, RM-EVAL achieves strong agreement with human rankings, and RGTG yields consistent gains in reward and external validation, demonstrating a unified framework for both assessing and enhancing GEC systems without relying on gold references.

</details>


### [109] [When Does Reasoning Help in Machine Translation? A Hierarchical Analysis of LRM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21247)
*Yuxiang Liu,Jiaming Luo,Eleftheria Briakou,Colin Cherry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究机器翻译中大推理模型中间推理轨迹的作用及其结构，提出层次化元总结（HMS）框架，发现推理效果取决于模型、推理长度和推理模式，不能被统一鼓励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型 increasingly 在机器翻译中生成中间推理轨迹，但目前尚不清楚这些推理何时能够提升翻译质量、何时反而造成负面影响。现有研究也缺乏对推理语言、长度及功能结构的系统分析，因此需要跨模型、语言、领域和数据集研究推理轨迹的有效性与组织规律。

Method: 作者跨多个模型、语言、领域和数据集分析机器翻译推理轨迹，重点考察推理语言、推理长度及结构与翻译质量之间的关系。为发现轨迹中的重复功能模式，提出无需预定义分类体系的层次化元总结（Hierarchical Meta-Summarization, HMS）框架，分别归纳粗粒度和细粒度的推理结构。

Result: 研究发现：最佳推理语言具有模型特异性；推理长度与翻译质量呈非单调关系，过长推理并不必然带来更好结果；推理轨迹中存在反复出现的功能模式。HMS揭示出跨任务共享的组织结构，主要包括理解/规划、翻译/起草以及润色/验证，同时也存在领域特定的变化。

Conclusion: 机器翻译中的推理不应被一律鼓励，而应进行模型感知、长度感知和模式感知的控制。实践中需要针对不同模型选择合适的推理语言，控制推理长度，并利用推理功能结构来提升推理轨迹的有效性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models increasingly use intermediate traces for machine translation, but it remains unclear when such reasoning helps or hurts. We analyze reasoning traces across models, languages, domains, and datasets, focusing on reasoning language, length, and structure. We find that the best reasoning language is model-specific, reasoning length has a non-monotonic relationship with quality, and traces exhibit recurring functional patterns. To uncover these patterns, we introduce Hierarchical Meta-Summarization (HMS), a scalable framework that induces coarse- and fine-grained reasoning structures without predefined taxonomies. HMS reveals a shared organization--understanding/planning, translating/drafting, and refining/verifying--alongside domain-specific variation. Our results suggest that MT reasoning should be controlled in a model-aware, length-aware, and pattern-aware manner rather than uniformly encouraged.

</details>


### [110] [How Many Humans Is a Judge Panel Worth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21277)
*Chao Li,Yingying Yu,Yunfeng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型评审 panel 的“有效人类判断数”如何依赖评价目标。作者区分谱残差多样性与分布恢复误差，提出分别对应的有效规模 ν_H 和 ν_MSE，并在 ChaosNLI 三项任务上发现二者明显不一致，说明 panel 质量不能用单一有效规模或一般化的人类替代率衡量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评审 panel 常把多个模型的投票表现压缩为相对于单一金标准的二元误差，但这种做法会掩盖真实人类标注中的分歧与标签分布。论文旨在回答一个核心问题：一组语言模型评审究竟相当于多少个人类判断？作者特别关注这一答案是否会因匹配目标不同而改变，并希望建立能够审计评审 panel 的更细致指标。

Method: 作者将模型评审 panel 的分类投票与经验人类标签分布进行比较，保留模型间及人类标注中的分歧信息。首先，对归一化残差 Gram 矩阵进行谱分析，并将其参与比（participation ratio）与条件独立的人类参考抽样相匹配，得到谱多样性有效规模 ν_H。其次，单独匹配分布平方误差，得到 ν_MSE。论文进一步给出区分特征值、成员能量和平均方向权重的谱恒等式；构造可实现的硬标签 panel，分析在成员能量相等、相关性非负时谱多样性与分布恢复效果可能背离的情况；还考察不同 panel 规模内的排序稳定性、跨样本半区的一致性，以及共识方向在中心化残差方差中的占比 γ_co。

Result: 在 ChaosNLI 的三个任务中，相同的 32 个评审 panel 得到 ν_H=4.24–6.50，而 ν_MSE=2.30–3.75，表明谱多样性有效规模通常高于分布误差意义下的有效规模。实验显示，更高的谱多样性并不保证更好的标签分布恢复，即使成员能量相同且相关性为非负。不同任务中，固定 panel 大小下的成员排序一致性差异显著；某些成员的加入会对不同指标产生冲突影响，且这种冲突在两个项目子集上仍然存在。共识方向占中心化残差方差的比例为：MNLI-m 43.8%，SNLI 33.7%。

Conclusion: “有效规模”是一个依赖目标的测量，而非 panel 的单一固有属性。谱残差多样性与人类标签分布恢复能力不可互换，不能据此直接推导一般化的人类替代率。评估语言模型评审 panel 时，应明确目标函数，分别报告相应指标，并使用对齐后的投票数据与分析协议进行审计。

Abstract: How many human judgments does a panel of language models represent? The answer depends on what is matched. We audit categorical judge panels against empirical human label distributions, retaining disagreement that binary errors relative to one gold label collapse. We measure spectral residual diversity by matching the participation ratio of a normalized residual Gram matrix to conditionally independent human-reference draws, giving nu_H. We separately match distributional squared error, giving nu_MSE. Across three ChaosNLI tasks, the same 32-judge panels have nu_H=4.24--6.50 but nu_MSE=2.30--3.75. A spectral identity separates the eigenvalues, member energies, and averaging-direction weights that determine error. Realizable hard-label panels show that greater spectral diversity can accompany worse distribution recovery even with equal member energies and nonnegative correlations. In the observed panels, within-size ranking agreement varies sharply by task; some member additions produce conflicting changes that persist across two item halves. The consensus-direction share of centered residual variance is gamma_co=43.8% on MNLI-m and 33.7% on SNLI, quantifying shared variation retained by averaging. We provide aligned votes and analysis protocols for auditing these distinctions. Effective size is therefore a target-specific measurement: spectral diversity and distribution recovery should not be treated as interchangeable measures of panel quality or as general human-replacement rates.

</details>


### [111] [From Memory to Behavior: A Behavior-Aware Role-Playing Framework for Social Media Influencers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21349)
*Ji-Lun Peng,Yi-Zhen Zhang,Chun-Nan Chou,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向角色扮演的 Situation–Internal state–Behavior（SIB）Persona 方法，通过显式建模情境、内部状态与行为策略，提升语言模型对真实人物和虚构角色的回应拟真度；同时提出带有被模仿对象参考信息的评测协议，以改善对不知名人物的自动评价。实验表明，该方法优于现有基于上下文学习的基线，且评测协议与人工判断具有中等相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽然能够扮演真实人物，但难以忠实模仿其在不同情境下的反应方式。基于上下文学习的方法通常无法充分捕捉人物随情境变化而产生的行为策略。此外，对于不知名人物，缺乏足够背景信息的语言模型评估者难以可靠判断模仿质量。因此，研究需要同时改进情境依赖的行为建模与自动评测机制。

Method: 提出 Situation–Internal state–Behavior Persona 方法，将情境、人物内部状态及其行为策略纳入角色人格建模，使模型能够生成更具情境适应性的回应。研究还设计了一套评测协议，为语言模型评估者提供与被模仿人物相关的参考资料，从而辅助评价角色扮演的忠实度。方法在新构建的社交媒体回复生成数据集上进行评估，并进一步在虚构角色基准上验证其跨场景适用性。

Result: 在社交媒体回复生成任务中，所提出的方法超过了当前先进的基于上下文学习的基线方法。所提出的评测协议与人工判断达到中等程度的相关性，说明其能够在一定程度上替代或辅助人工评价。虚构角色基准实验进一步表明，该方法不仅适用于真实人物，也适用于虚构角色。

Conclusion: 显式引入情境相关的行为信息能够广泛提升角色扮演的忠实度，尤其适用于真实人物在社交媒体中的回应生成，也适用于虚构角色扮演。研究同时说明，为自动评估器提供人物参考信息，是改善不知名人物角色扮演评价有效性的可行途径。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong potential as role-playing agents for real individuals, yet faithful impersonating remains challenging. Existing in-context learning-based methods fail to capture how individuals react under different situations. In addition, LLM-based evaluation is difficult for obscure individuals. To address these challenges, we propose Situation--Internal state--Behavior Persona method to incorporate situation-dependent behavioral strategies. We further design an evaluation protocol that provides LLM evaluators with references about the impersonated individual. We evaluate our approach on a newly constructed dataset for the task of generating replies on social media. Experimental results show that our proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art ICL-based baselines, while our evaluation protocol achieves moderate correlation with human judgment. Besides, experiments on fictional-character benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed method is applicable beyond the social media setting. These findings suggest that incorporating behavioral information broadly improves the fidelity of role-playing for real individuals on social media or fictional characters.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Atomic Tokens: Factorizing Syllables for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21362)
*Nghia Hieu Nguyen,Thai Bao Huynh,Binh-An Dinh-Le,Phu Gia Hoang,Dat Tien Nguyen,Kiet Van Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向越南语和中文的音系分词器，将每个音节转换为国际音标（IPA），并分解为声母、韵母和声调三个共享同一上下文位置的组件；同时提出基于该分词器的 PhonemicBERT，通过组件嵌入和三个预测头重建被掩码音节。实验表明，该方法以极小词表获得更高的 Rényi 效率、越南语字典条目的 Fertility 恰为 1，并在中文和越南语多项语言理解任务上达到具有竞争力或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统字符或统计子词分词器忽略音节内部的音系结构，通常需要较大的词表，并可能造成序列冗余或跨音系相关单元之间的表示共享不足。中文和越南语以音节为重要语言单位，因此论文旨在设计一种语言学驱动、紧凑、可解释且无需语料依赖词表学习的分词方案。

Method: 首先，将中文和越南语的每个音节转换为 IPA，并确定性地分解为声母（onset）、韵母（rime）和声调（tone）三个音系组件。三个组件共同占据一个上下文位置，从而保持音节级序列长度，同时允许音系相关音节共享组件表示。对于非音系单位和不支持的单位，采用字符级回退机制。该方法分别使用 112 个中文词表条目和 256 个越南语词表条目。在模型层面，构建 PhonemicBERT：使用因子化组件嵌入，并通过三个预测头联合重建完整的被掩码音节。实验包括 Rényi efficiency、Fertility、序列长度比较，以及受控中文预训练和越南语预训练后的多项语言理解任务评估。

Result: 分词器在中文和越南语上均取得显著更高的 Rényi efficiency；越南语标准音节词典中的每个条目均以 Fertility=1 表示，并且通常生成比现有预训练分词器更短的越南语序列。在受控中文预训练中，PhonemicBERT-Zh 相较字符、子词和 SubChar 基线，在多种语言理解任务上表现具有竞争力或更优。PhonemicBERT-Vi 相较已有越南语模型和多语模型，也取得具有竞争力或更好的结果。

Conclusion: 音系分解能够作为原子化表示和统计分词之外的一种紧凑、高效且可解释的替代方案。该方法在不依赖语料库学习大词表的情况下，保留音节级序列结构并促进跨音系相关单元的表示共享，证明了语言学知识可以有效提升中文和越南语预训练模型的分词与理解能力。

Abstract: Conventional tokenizers represent text as characters or statistically derived subwords, overlooking the internal phonological structure of syllables and often requiring large vocabularies. We introduce \textbf{Phonemic Tokenizer}, a linguistically motivated tokenizer for Vietnamese and Chinese that converts each syllable into IPA and factorizes it into three phonological components: onset, rime, and tone. The three components jointly occupy one contextual position, preserving syllable-level sequence length while enabling representation sharing across phonologically related syllables. Non-phonological and unsupported units are handled through character-level fallback. This deterministic design requires no corpus-dependent vocabulary learning and yields vocabularies of only 112 entries for Chinese and 256 for Vietnamese. Intrinsic evaluation shows that the tokenizer achieves substantially higher Rényi efficiency in both languages, represents every entry in a standard Vietnamese syllable dictionary with a Fertility of exactly one, and generally produces shorter Vietnamese sequences than existing pretrained tokenizers. We further instantiate the tokenizer in \textbf{PhonemicBERT}, which combines factorized component embeddings and reconstructs complete masked syllables using three prediction heads. Under a controlled Chinese pretraining setup, PhonemicBERT-Zh is competitive with or outperforms character, subword, and SubChar alternatives across diverse language-understanding tasks. PhonemicBERT-Vi also achieves competitive or superior results to established Vietnamese and multilingual pretrained models. These results establish phonemic factorization as a compact, efficient, and interpretable alternative to atomic and statistically segmented text representations.

</details>


### [113] [ArenaFlow: From Trajectory Ranking to Hierarchical Credit Propagation for Open-Ended Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21378)
*Qiang Zhang,Ruixue Ding,Fanrui Zhang,Xi Chen,Boli Chen,Shihang Wang,Yinfeng Huang,Yi Zheng,Pengjun Xie,Kaipeng Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ArenaFlow 是一种面向开放式智能体强化学习的分层信用传播框架：利用锦标赛式相对排序获得轨迹级奖励，再将反馈传递到关键步骤和可复用技能，并通过技能记忆促进后续探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式智能体任务的解法多样，难以获得可靠的标量奖励。现有成对比较方法虽然缓解了奖励区分坍缩问题，但通常把丰富的比较反馈压缩成单一轨迹级奖励，无法识别决定性中间步骤，也难以将成功行为沉淀为可复用技能。因此，需要一种能够细粒度分配信用、同时管理和复用技能的强化学习方法。

Method: ArenaFlow 首先通过基于锦标赛的相对排名生成轨迹级奖励。随后结合结构化反思评估，提取三类监督信号：关键成功步骤、可复用策略技能，以及检索技能的使用归因。在步骤层面，根据轨迹在锦标赛中的存活深度，将轨迹级优势传播给高置信度的关键步骤，以优化局部推理行为。在技能层面，利用群体级使用归因估计技能效用，并通过效用感知的更新、剪枝和检索维护全局技能记忆；高效用技能还被用作后续探索的策略先验。

Result: 在开放式智能体任务上的大量实验表明，ArenaFlow 能够有效提升智能体强化学习性能。摘要未提供具体任务、基线、指标或数值，因此无法进一步判断其相对提升幅度、统计显著性和各组件的独立贡献。

Conclusion: ArenaFlow 将轨迹级相对评价、关键步骤信用分配和技能级记忆管理结合起来，形成面向开放式任务的分层强化学习机制。其核心价值在于把粗粒度的比较反馈转化为步骤级和技能级监督，从而实现更精准的行为优化与成功经验复用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has substantially improved large language model (LLM) agents in verifiable domains, but remains difficult to apply to open-ended agent tasks, where solutions are diverse and reliable scalar rewards are hard to obtain. Recent pairwise evaluation methods alleviate reward discrimination collapse by replacing pointwise scoring with relative preferences. However, they still compress rich comparative feedback into a single trajectory-level reward, obscuring decisive intermediate steps and preventing successful behaviors from being consolidated into reusable skills. We propose ArenaFlow, a hierarchical credit propagation framework for open-ended agent reinforcement learning. ArenaFlow leverages tournament-based relative ranking to derive trajectory-level reward signals. Each comparison is further equipped with structured reflective evaluation, which reveals three types of supervision: pivotal success steps, reusable strategy skills, and usage attribution of retrieved skills. At the step level, ArenaFlow propagates trajectory-level advantages to high-confidence pivotal steps according to tournament survival depth, enabling more targeted optimization of local reasoning behaviors. At the skill level, ArenaFlow estimates skill utility from group-level usage attribution and maintains a global skill memory through utility-aware updating, pruning, and retrieval. The resulting high-utility skills further serve as policy priors for future exploration. Extensive experiments validate ArenaFlow's effectiveness on open-ended agent tasks.

</details>


### [114] [Prediction Dynamics in Depth-Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21383)
*Xinyue Luo,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究深度递归语言模型中“答案已稳定但分数仍变化”的现象，提出基于边际几何的分解方法，说明分数更新可分为共同平移、相对获胜候选方向以及与竞争者分数间隔相关的配对项；实验表明，纳入方向和竞争者配对信息后，可进一步显著提前判定答案稳定深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度递归语言模型通过多次潜变量更新逐步 refined 预测，但中间步骤可能已经与最终答案一致，候选分数却仍持续变化。传统幅度界通常过于保守，无法解释这种“答案保持与分数变化并存”的现象。因此，本文旨在刻画哪些分数变化不会改变获胜答案，以及如何更准确地确定有限深度下的答案保持时刻。

Method: 作者推导了一个精确的边际刻画，将保守性分解为三部分：所有候选共同经历的平移、相对于当前获胜者的更新方向，以及每个竞争候选的更新与其分数间隔之间的配对关系。在 Huginn-3.5B 和 Ouro-1.4B 上进行回顾性轨迹分析，并分别考察完整答案文本评分与标签评分。对于共享预测分布的情形，作者进一步将更新正交分解为共同运动和对比运动，并用候选集合质量及集合内集中度表示共同分量。

Result: 在完整答案文本评分下，相比仅移除共同平移，进一步考虑更新方向和竞争者配对后，平均最早满足条件的深度又减少了总深度的 22.5%–34.4%。标签评分下也观察到实质性贡献。理论分析显示，共同能量与对比能量可以以不同速率衰减，因此即使绝对更新幅度下降，偏好变化所占比例仍可能上升。

Conclusion: 答案在有限深度内保持不变，并不意味着所有分数都停止变化。决定答案稳定性的关键是分数更新的几何结构及其组成，尤其是共同平移、相对方向和竞争者间隔配对。该框架比单纯依赖更新幅度的界更精确地解释了中间答案与最终答案的一致性。

Abstract: Depth-recurrent language models refine predictions through repeated latent updates. Why can intermediate answers agree with the endpoint while their scores continue to change? We derive a sharp margin characterization that decomposes the conservatism of a magnitude bound into common translation, direction relative to the winner, and the pairing of each competitor's update with its score gap. Across Huginn-3.5B and Ouro-1.4B, accounting for update direction and competitor pairing reduces the mean earliest qualifying depth by a further 22.5-34.4% of the total depth beyond translation removal under full answer-text scoring. This retrospective comparison uses completed trajectories. Substantial contributions also occur under label scoring. For shared predictive distributions, we separate common and contrast motion orthogonally and express the common component through candidate-set mass and within-set concentration. Common and contrast energies can attenuate at different rates, allowing a growing preference-change share to coexist with shrinking absolute updates. These findings explain finite-depth answer preservation through the geometry and composition of observed score changes.

</details>


### [115] [Consistent Relexicalization of Clinical Documents using Graph-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21387)
*Dipankar Das,Atri Mandal,Sandeep Singh,Tushar Shandhilya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: G-RELIC 是一种面向临床自然语言处理的图驱动上下文重词化方法，通过实体一一映射与确定性时间重定位，提升合成临床数据的关系完整性和时间一致性，同时维持隐私保护水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床数据重词化方法通常独立替换实体，难以保持纵向病历中的结构完整性、实体关系一致性和时间逻辑，导致合成数据不适合科学分析。论文旨在提高重词化数据的分析价值，并降低再识别风险。

Method: G-RELIC 结合大语言模型与图结构：利用基于图的映射机制，优化原始实体与替代实体之间的一一对应关系；同时采用确定性时间重定位算法，维持跨记录的时间一致性。方法在多种真实世界临床数据集上与现有先进基线进行比较，并评估关系完整性、时间连贯性及隐私指标。

Result: 实验显示，G-RELIC 在不损害既有临床数据隐私基准的前提下，显著优于基线。关系完整性由62.1%提升至92.5%，提高30.4个百分点；时间连贯性由46%提升至91.9%，提高45.9个百分点。

Conclusion: G-RELIC 能有效解决临床数据重词化中的实体关系断裂与时间不一致问题，在保持隐私保护的同时显著增强合成数据的纵向一致性和科研分析效用。

Abstract: Relexicalization is a pivotal technique in clinical NLP, as it facilitates robust masking of sensitive information while synthesizing datasets that retain high-fidelity, real-world characteristics. However, preserving structural integrity, relational coherence, and temporal consistency during transformation remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches frequently rely on independent entity replacement, which results in clinical inconsistencies across longitudinal records. This reduces the value of such relexicalized datasets for downstream scientific analysis. To address these limitations, we introduce G-RELIC (Graph Based Contextual Relexicalization with Improved Consistency) which combines the power of LLMs with graphs. G-RELIC implements a graph-based mapping mechanism which optimizes for one-to-one correspondence between original and surrogate entities. It also introduces a deterministic temporal repositioning algorithm to preserve temporal consistency. Empirical evaluations on diverse, real-world clinical datasets validate that G-RELIC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. G-RELIC yields a 30.4 percentage point improvement in relational integrity (62.1% to 92.5%) and 45.9 percentage point improvement in temporal coherence (46% to 91.9%) without compromising on the recognized privacy benchmarks for clinical datasets. This maximizes the analytical utility of relexicalized datasets while minimizing re-identification risk.

</details>


### [116] [Omni Demand Understanding: A Benchmark for Contextual User-Intent Inference in Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21392)
*Qi Chen,Yunfei Chu,Haolin He,Yifan Yang,Zihan Liu,Yuxuan Wang,Ziyang Ma,Ruiyang Xu,Meng Gao,Yinsong Yan,Ling Wang,Hui Wang,Wen Huang,Yiheng Chen,Guanrou Yang,Qiuqiang Kong,Jin Xu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Omni Demand Understanding（ODU）这一新的多模态上下文推理任务，并构建 ODU-Bench 评测模型能否从音频、视觉与对话历史中判断用户是否真的提出需求以及具体意图。对14个原生多模态大模型的评测显示，当前模型在关键上下文信息恢复和避免误触发方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态交互基准主要评价模型生成回复的质量，却较少关注更基础的前置能力：模型能否正确识别用户是否有真实需求，并从含糊、口语化、表达不流畅、噪声环境以及多轮对话中推断其潜在意图。尤其是形式上类似请求的话语可能并非面向助手，错误触发会导致不恰当响应。因此，论文旨在系统评估模型在真实自然交互中理解用户需求的能力。

Method: 论文将 ODU 定义为一个多模态上下文推理问题：给定连续交互流，模型需要判断是否存在用户需求，并结合视觉、声学和对话上下文推断意图。评测覆盖五个维度，兼顾单轮与多轮交互。作者基于挑战驱动的分类体系设计 ODU-Bench，采用分类体系引导的智能体视频生成与真人录制交互构造数据，再进行基于媒体内容的标注和人工复核。随后评测14个原生多模态大模型。

Result: 最强模型 Gemini 3.1 Pro 只能恢复约44.7%的、必须依赖视觉、声学或对话上下文才能推断出的关键信息。14个模型中有11个在非需求场景上的误触发率超过50%。结果表明，当前多模态大模型不仅难以完整提取隐含需求信息，也难以可靠地区分真正需求与表面上类似请求的非需求表达。

Conclusion: ODU揭示了当前多模态大模型在生成回复之前的需求理解环节存在系统性能力缺口。论文主张将“先判断是否有需求、再理解其意图”作为多模态交互中的独立且关键评测能力，并通过 ODU-Bench 为后续研究提供基准。

Abstract: Natural audio-visual interaction is emerging as an important interface for AI assistants, allowing users to communicate through speech and vision rather than carefully composed text prompts. However, existing benchmarks of interactive capabilities still focus primarily on response quality, leaving a more fundamental question underexplored: can a model correctly infer the user's underlying demand from complex multimodal interaction? Real-world user demands are often underspecified in speech and must be inferred from multimodal cues and dialogue history. This inference is further complicated by ambiguous or disfluent expression and noisy acoustic environments. Conversely, request-like speech may not constitute a demand to the assistant, leading to false triggers. We establish Omni Demand Understanding (ODU) as a distinct multimodal contextual inference problem: given an interaction stream, a model must detect whether a user demand is present and infer intent from multimodal and conversational context. ODU evaluates this capability along five dimensions, covering both single-turn and multi-turn interactions. We construct ODU-Bench using a challenge-driven taxonomy, taxonomy-guided agentic video generation, and human-recorded interactions, followed by media-grounded annotation and human verification. We evaluate 14 native MLLMs. Even the strongest, Gemini 3.1 Pro, recovers only 44.7% of key information that must be inferred from visual, acoustic, or conversational context. Moreover, 11 of the 14 models exhibit false-trigger rates above 50% on non-demand scenarios. These results reveal a systematic capability gap in current MLLMs' ability to infer contextual user demands. We hope ODU can establish the evaluation of a previously underexplored yet essential capability in multimodal interaction: correctly understanding user demands before generating an appropriate response.

</details>


### [117] [Talking Past the Machine: Morality, Politeness, and Alignment in Human-AI Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21401)
*Marina Mitiaeva,Lu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较人类—ChatGPT与人类—人类多轮对话中的合作性沟通机制，发现AI能够生成礼貌、道德和语言协调等表层特征，但缺乏人类互动中的相互适应与社会协商结构；部分促进人类合作的机制在AI对话中甚至呈现反向作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话式AI能够产生流畅且 socially appropriate 的回应，但其是否真正参与合作沟通，还是仅仅模拟合作沟通的表面形式，仍不明确。该问题直接关系到AI系统的评估、可信度与设计。研究因此聚焦于道德表达、礼貌和互动对齐这三个合作对话的核心维度，并比较其在人类—AI和人类—人类互动中的作用机制。

Method: 研究分析15,881段人类—ChatGPT多轮对话和10,784段人类—人类多轮对话，使用混合效应模型识别哪些沟通特征能够预测相邻轮次之间的对齐程度。重点考察道德表达、礼貌相关特征、语言趋同、缓和表达、纯洁性框架以及赋予用户互动塑造空间的能动性等因素。

Result: 研究发现，AI能够稳定地产生合作沟通的表层特征，但缺乏相应的社会互动结构：道德输出更像预先配置，而非互动协商；AI能够表达温暖，却未必体现对对方面子需求的敏感性；语言趋同持续下降。更重要的是，部分机制在AI互动中发生反转：在人类对话中通常促进适应的模糊化和缓和表达，在AI表达时反而与较低对齐相关；在人类对话中通常伴随分歧的纯洁性框架，却与用户向AI趋同相关。赋予用户塑造交流过程的能动性，是两类互动中最稳定的对齐预测因素。较新模型道德断言程度降低，也没有同步带来更强的合作性。

Conclusion: AI主要复现了合作沟通的表面形式，而没有复现支撑人类合作的相互适应机制。某些在人类互动中有效的合作线索，在人类—AI互动中可能反向运行，说明仅以单轮或逐轮对齐评估对话质量可能不足，应进一步采用互动层面的指标，并重视用户能动性与长期适应过程。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems produce fluent, socially appropriate responses, yet whether they participate in cooperative communication or merely simulate its surface forms remains unclear - a question central to how these systems are evaluated, trusted, and designed. This study investigates how morality, politeness, and alignment - three dimensions central to cooperative dialogue - function in human-AI interaction compared to human-human conversation. We analyze 15,881 human-ChatGPT and 10,784 human-human multi-turn dialogues, using mixed-effects models to identify which features predict turn-to-turn alignment. We observe a consistent dissociation: AI produces the surface features of cooperative communication without the underlying social architecture. Moral output appears preconfigured rather than negotiated; warmth is generated without face sensitivity; linguistic convergence declines persistently. Most strikingly, the cooperative mechanisms themselves reverse direction: hedging and softening associated with greater accommodation between humans are associated with reduced alignment when produced by AI, and purity framing associated with human divergence coincides with users converging toward the AI. Agency - giving users room to shape the exchange - is the most consistent predictor of alignment across both interaction types, while lower moral assertiveness in more recent models is not accompanied by better cooperation. Together these patterns suggest that AI reproduces the surface of cooperation without the mutual adaptation that grounds it between humans - and, more surprisingly, that mechanisms sustaining human accommodation can run in reverse with AI, suggesting a turn-level view may be insufficient for interaction-level success.

</details>


### [118] [Benchmarking Gender Bias in Machine Translation Evaluation Metrics across Occupations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21490)
*Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Giorgos Filandrianos,Wafaa Mohammed,Giuseppe Attanasio,Chrysoula Zerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究机器翻译自动评价中的性别偏差。基于GAMBIT+职业均衡数据集，比较男性与女性译文在不同语言、职业和评价系统中的得分及错误标注差异。结果表明，男性译文总体更容易获得较高评价，但偏差程度因评价器、语言和职业而异，因此需要从方向、幅度和频率等多个维度衡量偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译中的性别偏差不仅体现在生成结果中，也体现在自动评价环节。当英语源文本未明确指示人物性别时，目标语言可能必须选择阳性或阴性形式；此时，翻译系统和评价指标若系统性偏好某一性别，就会造成缺乏源文依据的评价不公平。现有单一总体指标难以充分揭示这种偏差，因此本文旨在系统考察不同评价器和语言中的性别偏差表现。

Method: 作者在WMT 2026自动翻译质量评价系统共享任务中，使用GAMBIT+的职业均衡子集，覆盖英语到阿拉伯语、捷克语、希腊语、冰岛语、俄语、乌克兰语和德语的七个语言方向。每种目标语言包含1,308对男性/女性译文，涵盖436个ISCO-08职业类别，每个职业提供三个例子。研究同时评估质量分数预测系统和错误标注系统，并从偏好方向、偏差幅度和出现频率三个维度分析性别差异，同时考察不同职业和语言的变化。

Result: 总体上，男性译文获得更高评价分数的倾向较为明显。不同职业之间的差异与社会刻板印象相关，但这种偏好在不同评价器和语言之间的强度与一致性差异较大。研究还表明，仅观察单一聚合指标不足以完整刻画评价器的性别偏差。

Conclusion: 机器翻译自动评价中仍存在性别偏差，评价系统可能在源文本未提供性别依据时偏向男性表达。准确评估偏差需要结合偏好方向、幅度、频率以及语言和职业维度进行多角度分析，而不能依赖单一总体统计量。

Abstract: Gender bias remains a persistent concern in machine translation (MT), affecting both generated translations and their automatic evaluation. When a source text leaves a person's gender unspecified, translations may realize that person using masculine or feminine forms, and both MT systems and evaluation metrics may exhibit systematic preferences between these alternatives despite the source providing no basis for such a distinction. We study this behavior in the WMT 2026 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Systems Shared Task using an occupation-balanced subset of GAMBIT+. We consider seven English-source language pairs, six from the original dataset, targeting Arabic, Czech, Greek, Icelandic, Russian, and Ukrainian, and extend the original resource with German. The subset contains 1,308 masculine/feminine translation pairs per target language, with three examples for each of the 436 ISCO-08 occupational groups. We evaluate shared-task submissions and baselines for score prediction and error annotation, examining the direction, magnitude, and frequency of gender-related differences. We find an overall tendency for masculine translations to receive higher scores, as well as differences per occupation following stereotypical gender representations, although the strength and consistency of this preference vary considerably across evaluators and languages. Our results show that gender bias remains present in MT evaluation, but that capturing its extent requires looking beyond a single aggregate measure to complementary dimensions of evaluator behavior.

</details>


### [119] [MIRAGE: Multi-Perspective Creative Language Model Reasoning with Reinforcement Learning Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21554)
*Arash Lagzian,Srinivas Anumasa,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAGE，一种通过多概念视角切换和智能体引导探索来提升大语言模型复杂推理能力的推理时框架。实验表明，该方法在多个数学、科学、逻辑与组合推理基准上优于链式思维和多样化提示集成方法，并仅增加较小的推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型取得显著进展，但在复杂数学、科学和逻辑任务上仍存在推理能力不足的问题。受人类认知灵活性启发，本文希望让模型能够动态切换不同的思考视角，从而突破单一推理路径的局限，提高复杂任务的求解准确率与可靠性。

Method: 提出MIRAGE（Multi-perspective Inference-time Reasoning via Agent-Guided Exploration）框架。该框架包含两个核心模块：Selector负责优先选择更有效的概念视角，例如代数视角和概率视角；Reasoner负责基于选定视角按序求解任务。当获得高置信度答案时停止推理，否则整合多个视角的结果。该方法主要在推理阶段工作，无需改变模型参数。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH500、MMLU-Pro和Game-of-24等基准上的实验显示，MIRAGE持续优于Chain-of-Thought和多样化提示集成等方法，显著提升任务准确率，同时仅引入较小的推理开销，体现出较好的可扩展性与实用潜力。

Conclusion: 动态选择并组合多个概念推理视角，可以有效增强大语言模型处理复杂任务的能力。MIRAGE为无需训练或少量额外推理成本的推理时增强提供了一种可行方案，适合进一步应用于数学、科学和逻辑推理场景。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized artificial intelligence and how human interact with AIs. Despite impressive advancements, LLMs struggle with complex mathematical, scientific, and logical tasks. Inspired by human cognitive flexibility - our ability to dynamically switch mental perspectives - we propose MIRAGE (Multi-perspective Inference-time Reasoning via Agent-Guided Exploration), a novel inference-time creative thinking framework. MIRAGE includes a Selector that prioritizes effective conceptual perspectives (e.g., algebraic, probabilistic) and a Reasoner that sequentially solves tasks until a confident solution emerges, otherwise aggregating multiple perspectives. Tested on GSM8K, MATH500, MMLU-Pro, and Game-of-24 benchmarks, MIRAGE consistently outperforms methods like Chain-of-Thought and diverse prompting ensembles, significantly boosting accuracy with minimal inference overhead, providing a scalable solution for practical applications.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluating In-Context Learning and Retrieval Strategies for Devanagari Post-OCR Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21595)
*Abhishek Bhandari,Gaurav Harit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估3B–32B大语言模型在印地语和马拉地语Devanagari文字OCR后纠错中的免训练上下文学习能力，重点比较三种示例检索策略，并提出字符n-gram BM25检索方法CharBM25。结果表明，检索策略和模型规模是决定性能的关键因素：字符三元组检索优于随机和密集语义检索，12B以上模型结合CharBM25可实现较可靠的免训练纠错，而8B以下模型收益不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未系统探索大语言模型对Devanagari文字，尤其是印地语和马拉地语，进行训练免费OCR后纠错的有效性。论文旨在回答两个问题：不同上下文示例检索策略如何影响纠错质量，以及模型规模是否决定少样本纠错能否稳定超过原始OCR结果。

Method: 构建覆盖五个新闻领域、约20,000个句子的印地语和马拉地语评测基准，评估3B–32B规模的大语言模型。比较三种上下文示例选择方式：领域随机选择、密集语义检索，以及提出的CharBM25。CharBM25在OCR输入上计算字符n-gram BM25相似度，以检索具有相似字符级错误模式的示例；同时比较字符unigram、bigram和trigram，并以词错误率（WER）衡量纠错效果。

Result: CharBM25相较领域随机选择在印地语上带来2.8–4.0个百分点的绝对WER改善，在马拉地语上带来2.9–3.8个百分点改善；字符三元组整体优于一元组和二元组。Gemma-3-27B结合CharBM25-5时，印地语WER降低55.0%，马拉地语降低33.3%。8B以下模型通常无法稳定优于OCR基线，3B模型在马拉地语中甚至会使更多句子变差。马拉地语在所有模型规模下都比印地语更难纠正。CharBM25达到或超过密集检索效果，同时计算成本很低且无需GPU。

Conclusion: 字符级错误模式检索是Devanagari OCR后纠错中的关键因素，CharBM25是一种高效、低成本且无需训练的上下文示例检索策略。结合12B及以上的通用大语言模型，可以实现较可靠的印地语和马拉地语OCR后纠错；但模型规模不足时，少样本提示的收益不稳定，且马拉地语因形态复杂度更高仍具有更大挑战。

Abstract: In-context learning using Large Language Models (LLMs) offers a compelling path to training-free post-OCR correction, yet its effectiveness for Devanagari script remains entirely unexplored. We present the first systematic evaluation of LLMs (3B-32B) for post-OCR correction in Hindi and Marathi, comparing three in-context example retrieval strategies: domain-random selection, dense semantic retrieval, and our proposed CharBM25, which retrieves examples by character n-gram BM25 similarity over OCR inputs to target shared error patterns with the test sentence. Across a 20,000-sentence benchmark spanning five news domains, retrieval strategy is the decisive factor in correction quality: CharBM25 outperforms domain-random selection by 2.8-4.0pp absolute WER on Hindi and 2.9-3.8pp on Marathi, using character trigrams, which consistently outperform bigrams and unigrams. Scale dominates performance: Gemma-3-27B achieves WER reductions of 55.0% for Hindi and 33.3% for Marathi under CharBM25-5. Few-shot gains are capacity-gated: models below 8B do not reliably improve over the OCR baseline, and on Marathi the smallest models (3B) degrade more sentences than they improve. Marathi is persistently harder to correct than Hindi across all scales, reflecting its greater morphological complexity. These findings establish CharBM25 as an effective, GPU-free retrieval strategy that matches or exceeds dense retrieval at negligible computational cost, and show that combining it with a general-purpose LLM of 12B+ parameters delivers reliable, training-free Devanagari post-OCR correction without task-specific fine-tuning. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/AbhishekBhandari/Devanagari-OCR-ICL-Benchmark

</details>


### [121] [Steering LLMs Responses Towards Moral Foundations on the Norwegian MFQ-30](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21636)
*Hans Andersen,David Dichas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估六个开放权重大语言模型在挪威道德基础问卷（MFQ-30）上的回答是否稳定、是否接近真实人类群体，并比较提示词人格引导与激活引导（ActAdd）的调控效果。结果显示，部分模型会出现近似人类均值但不随题目内容变化的“平坦/中心倾向”回答；对能够正常作答的模型，北欧受访者人格提示可使其道德基础分布接近挪威样本，而固定中层的一对ActAdd主要造成分布变平，未能精准调节单个道德基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用心理测量问卷刻画大语言模型的道德与价值 profile，但尚不清楚这些测量是否反映模型中稳定、可解释的特征，也不清楚模型的回答能否被引导至目标人类群体的分布。因此，本文以挪威道德基础问卷为例，检验模型回答的内容参与性、群体相似性以及可操控性。

Method: 研究者将MFQ-30施测于六个开放权重大语言模型，并与1,282名挪威受访者的道德基础分布比较。研究考察两种干预：一是提示词层面的角色/人格引导，二是激活层面的ActAdd。模型被分为接受注意力检查的条件与默认回答条件，以区分真正依据题目内容作答和产生平坦或中心倾向输出的模型。相似度使用Mahalanobis d²衡量。

Result: 部分模型在基线下会产生接近人类平均值、但不追踪题目内容的平坦或中心倾向回答。对于能够参与问卷内容的模型，一个不包含人类样本分布信息的中性北欧受访者人格提示，使其与挪威样本均值的Mahalanobis d²接近程度提升44%至77%。在固定中间层施加单对ActAdd后，道德基础 profile趋于变平，而不是对特定道德基础进行定向调节。此外，至少有一个模型在该人格提示下不仅改变了profile，还从基线的不参与转为参与问卷作答。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的问卷道德 profile 可能混合了稳定的价值表征、回答策略和是否真正理解题目的行为状态，不能仅凭接近人类均值就推断存在相应心理特征。人格提示比所测试的固定层ActAdd更能使部分模型接近目标人类群体，但其同时可能诱发新的参与行为，说明所谓的价值引导可能伴随状态切换或“认知幻象”，需要结合注意力检查和内容敏感性进行谨慎解释。

Abstract: Recent work applies human psychometric questionnaires to large language models to elicit moral and value profiles, but it is not clear whether these instruments measure anything stable in models or whether the resulting profiles can be moved toward a target human population. We administer the Norwegian Moral Foundations Questionnaire (MFQ-30) to six open-weight LLMs and compare their foundation profiles to a sample of N = 1,282 Norwegian respondents. We test two steering interventions, prompt-level persona steering and activation-level ActAdd. Half the models engage with the questionnaire under our attention check. The other half default to flat or central-tendency outputs that look near-human on average without tracking item content. A neutral Nordic-respondent persona, written without any distributional information from the human sample, brings the engaging models 44-77% closer to the Norwegian mean in Mahalanobis $d^2$. One-pair ActAdd at a fixed mid-layer flattens the foundation profile rather than steering individual foundations. For at least one model the same persona that shifts the profile also induces engagement that was absent at baseline, a concrete instance of the cognitive phantoms that Peereboom et al. (2025) warn about.

</details>


### [122] [Chinese Competitive Debating Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21637)
*Zongrui Yang,Haoyuan Li,Zhongsheng Wang,Zhirui Zeng,Pengqian Han,Yi Zhou,Yuting Wang,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向竞技中文辩论的大语言模型评测数据集与基准，覆盖比赛、阶段和辩手三个层级，并通过职业评委标注检验模型与人工裁判的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辩论数据集通常缺少真实比赛中的细粒度交互记录，也很少同时包含专业评委依据统一评分标准给出的比赛判决、阶段评分、最佳辩手票选和评语。因此，难以系统评估大语言模型对辩论动态发展、论证质量及裁判决策的理解能力。

Method: 研究者组织182场竞技中文辩论，邀请120名专业评委，每场由3名评委依据预先定义的评分标准独立裁判。剔除记录不完整的比赛后，形成包含148场比赛、2698个阶段和20542个交锋单元的数据集，并对转录文本及阶段切分进行人工核验。基于这些数据设置三项任务：比赛胜负倾向预测、阶段得分预测和最佳辩手预测；同时进行多个大语言模型的零样本评测。

Result: 最终数据集保留原始阶段得分、比赛投票、最佳辩手票选及裁判评语。零样本实验中，最佳比赛胜负预测准确率为66.2%，模型阶段得分与人工平均评分的最高皮尔逊相关系数为0.250，最佳辩手预测最高准确率为56.8%。

Conclusion: 该数据集和基准为研究大语言模型理解交互式论证、追踪辩论发展过程以及与专业裁判判断的一致性提供了系统化测试平台。结果表明，模型在比赛层面的胜负倾向预测具有一定能力，但在细粒度阶段评分和最佳辩手识别方面，与专业评委仍存在明显差距。

Abstract: Debate adjudication requires tracking how arguments develop through interaction, yet existing datasets rarely combine fine-grained debate transcripts with professional judgments collected during real competitions under a shared rubric. We introduce a dataset and benchmark for evaluating large language models' understanding of competitive Chinese-language debate at the match, stage, and speaker levels. We organized 182 matches and recruited 120 professional judges, with each match independently adjudicated by three judges using a predefined rubric. After excluding matches with incomplete records, the dataset contains 148 matches, 2,698 stages, and 20,542 exchange units, with manually verified transcripts and segmentation. It preserves original stage scores, match votes, best-debater ballots, and adjudication rationales. We define three tasks: winner-tendency prediction, stage-score prediction, and best-debater prediction. Zero-shot evaluation of multiple large language models yields a highest winner-prediction accuracy of 66.2%, a highest Pearson correlation of 0.250 between model stage scores and mean human ratings, and a highest best-debater prediction accuracy of 56.8%. The dataset and benchmark provide a testbed for studying large language models' understanding of interactive argumentation and their agreement with professional judges.

</details>


### [123] [Analysing the Linearity of Linguistic Relations in Language Model Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21655)
*Vasudevan Nedumpozhimana,Fathima Thekkekara,John Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于衡量语言模型嵌入空间中不同语言关系线性可编码程度的统一框架，并在扩展版 BATS 数据集及 GloVe、RoBERTa、ModernBERT 上进行比较。结果表明，屈折和派生关系几乎可以完美线性编码，而词汇语义关系和百科知识关系的线性误差明显更高，尤其是一对多和多对多关系；RoBERTa 与 ModernBERT 通常优于 GloVe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常通过类比任务或向量运算考察嵌入中的关系，但缺乏一种能够系统、定量比较不同关系线性可访问性的统一方法。本文旨在揭示哪些语言关系在嵌入几何空间中具有稳定的线性结构，并比较不同模型的关系表示能力。

Method: 作者将线性编码形式化为一个受约束的线性近似问题，联合使用相关词对与不相关词对来估计关系的线性表示误差。实验基于扩展版 BATS 数据集，覆盖屈折、派生、词汇语义和百科知识四类关系，并比较 GloVe、RoBERTa 与 ModernBERT 的表现，同时分析一对多和多对多关系对线性编码的影响。

Result: 屈折关系和派生关系表现出接近完美的线性编码；词汇语义关系和百科知识关系的误差显著更高，且一对多、多对多关系尤其难以线性表示。总体上，RoBERTa 和 ModernBERT 的关系线性度通常高于 GloVe。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效识别嵌入空间中不同关系的线性可访问性，为分析和比较语言模型的关系几何结构提供了简洁、统一的探测工具。研究也表明，形态关系比复杂的词汇语义和百科知识关系更适合用线性结构表示。

Abstract: We propose a framework to analyse how strongly different linguistic relations are linearly encoded in language model embedding spaces. We formalise linear encoding via a constrained linear approximation over related and unrelated word pairs and apply this to an extended BATS dataset covering inflectional, derivational, lexicographic, and encyclopedic relations in GloVe, RoBERTa, and ModernBERT. Our experiments show near-perfect linear encodings for inflectional and derivational relations, but substantially higher errors for lexicographic and encyclopedic relations, especially for one-to-many and many-to-many associations. We also find that RoBERTa and ModernBERT generally encode relations more linearly than GloVe. These results indicate that our framework can reveal which relational structures are most linearly accessible in embeddings, offering a compact tool for probing and comparing relational geometry across models.

</details>


### [124] [When Steering Fails in Latent Reasoning: A Latent-to-Language Transition Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21662)
*Gaoxiang Huang,Lei Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究激活引导在隐式思维（latent CoT）中的有效性，发现其对后续语言生成的控制明显弱于显式思维链，并将原因归结为“潜变量到语言”的过渡鸿沟。研究表明，思维表示中仍保留任务信息，但干预效果在生成接口处发生衰减，因此该过渡边界是未来隐式引导方法设计与评估的关键位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导已被广泛用于控制语言模型的显式思维链推理，但其能否同样有效地控制隐式、连续的思维表示尚不清楚。论文旨在解释一个核心现象：即使对隐藏表示施加了幅度相近的干预，隐式思维中的干预为何对后续语言生成产生的影响显著弱于显式思维链。

Method: 论文比较了显式CoT与隐式连续思维中的激活引导效果，并开展三类分析：第一，检验连续思维中是否仍可识别任务信息；第二，观察从隐式思维表示过渡到语言生成时输出分布是否发生突变；第三，评估任务相关方向在隐式与显式CoT中对生成结果的双向控制能力。基于这些实验，论文提出并验证“潜变量到语言过渡鸿沟”假设。

Result: 结果显示：连续思维中仍然包含可识别的任务信息，说明控制效果弱并非源于任务信息完全缺失；从隐式表示到语言生成的过渡边界处，输出分布出现突变；此外，任务相关方向在隐式CoT中的双向控制能力远弱于显式CoT。即使隐藏表示移动幅度相当，隐式思维干预也难以有效传递到最终语言输出。

Conclusion: 论文认为，隐式思维激活引导的主要瓶颈位于潜变量到语言的过渡接口，而非任务信息是否存在或表示移动幅度是否足够。未来的隐式引导方法应重点建模、优化并评估这一过渡边界，以提升隐藏思维干预对语言生成的可控性。

Abstract: Activation steering has become a widely used approach for controlling language models during explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, motivating its extension to latent CoT. However, we find that steering continuous thoughts produces substantially weaker effects on subsequent language generation than steering explicit CoT, even when the hidden representations are moved by comparable amounts. We first show that task information remains identifiable in continuous thoughts. Hence, we hypothesize a \textbf{latent-to-language transition gap}, in which an intervention effect in latent space fails to transfer to language generation. Two further results support this hypothesis: the output distribution changes abruptly at the transition boundary, and task-related directions exert much weaker bidirectional control in latent CoT than in explicit CoT. These findings identify the transition interface as a central target for evaluating and designing future latent-steering methods.

</details>


### [125] [Rethinking Human-Aligned Evaluation: An Analysis of Semantic Metrics Beyond WER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21663)
*Hritika Sharma,Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Alessandra Pinto,Petr Motlicek,Hyunggu Jung,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Somang Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出并使用 HATS-en 数据集，比较 WER、CER、BERTScore 和 SemDist 与人类对 ASR 转录质量判断的一致性。结果显示 WER 最不符合人类判断，最佳 SemDist 配置表现最好，CER 以低成本取得接近最佳结果，因此建议以 CER 为主要指标，并将 SemDist 作为补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ASR 评估主要依赖词错误率（WER），但 WER 将所有词汇偏差视为同等严重，无法区分是否真正改变语义，也未必符合人类对转录质量的感知。论文旨在检验 WER 是否能够反映人类判断，并寻找更符合人类中心评价需求的指标。

Method: 作者构建英语人类中心 ASR 评估数据集 HATS-en，并以该数据集中的人类评价作为参照，比较词法指标 WER、CER，以及多种 BERTScore 和 SemDist 配置。实验系统地改变语言模型、模型层和池化策略，以分析语义相似度指标在不同设置下的表现。

Result: WER 在所有测试指标中与人类判断的一致性最低。表现最好的 SemDist 配置取得最高的总体一致性，优于 CER 和 BERTScore；但不存在在所有实验设置下都最优的单一模型。CER 虽然简单、计算成本低，却与最佳 SemDist 配置的表现非常接近。

Conclusion: 论文支持将 ASR 评估从 WER 转向 CER，尤其适用于英语及形音文字系统，因为 CER 更易解释、成本更低，并且更接近评价实际应捕捉的内容。同时，SemDist 可作为 CER 的语义层面补充指标，用于进一步反映人类对转录质量的判断。

Abstract: Word Error Rate (WER), the most commonly used metric for Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR), treats every lexical deviation from the reference as equally costly, regardless of whether it changes meaning. This raises the question: does WER actually track how humans judge ASR transcript quality? We introduce HATS-en, an English dataset for human-centered ASR evaluation. Using this dataset, we benchmark lexical metrics against several configurations of BERTScore and SemDist, varying the language model, layer, and pooling strategy. We find that WER agrees least with human judgment among all metrics tested, that the best-performing SemDist configurations achieve the highest overall agreement, ahead of CER and BERTScore, and that no single model is best across settings. CER, despite its simplicity and low cost, remains remarkably close to these best configurations. In line with prior recommendations, our results support shifting ASR evaluation toward CER both for English and for morphosyllabic writing systems as it is a more interpretable and low-cost metric for what evaluation should actually capture, and using SemDist as a complementary evaluation.

</details>


### [126] [PRISM-BN: A Controlled Corpus and Benchmark for Text-to-Parameterized Bayesian Network Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21673)
*Amartya Bhattacharya,Nikhil Singh,Neeti Pokhriyal,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRISM-BN，一个用于评估从文本恢复参数化贝叶斯网络的受控语料库与基准。数据集包含 5054 个文本—离散 BN 配对样本，覆盖节点、状态、有向边、根节点先验及多父节点条件概率分布（CPD），并通过结构指标与严格 CPD 指标分别评估结构恢复和概率参数估计。实验表明节点识别和严格 CPD 恢复仍具挑战，而条件状态与边恢复较强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯网络具有明确的有向结构和概率参数，是神经符号人工智能的重要符号表示。然而，现有文本到参数化 BN 的训练与评测受到大规模配对文本—BN 资源缺乏的限制。作者因此需要构建一个可控、可重复、能够区分结构恢复与概率参数估计能力的基准。

Method: 作者提出 PRISM-BN 语料库和 PRISM 构建流程。语料库包含 5054 个基于 BN 的描述及其对应的离散参考 BN，覆盖五个领域，并由 50 个 Wikipedia 种子骨干网络扩展而来。PRISM 采用“边际分布优先”的流程，获取边际分布与局部联合分布，解析恢复归一化 CPD，并构造局部重新参数化的子图。评测方面，作者使用语义节点与状态对齐、条件结构评分以及严格完整 CPD 评价，对六个 LLM 提取器进行比较；同时使用独立生成的 GPT-5.5 参考结果和人工试验进行稳健性验证。

Result: 六个 LLM 提取器的 Node F1 为 0.56–0.83，条件 Edge F1 为 0.90–0.97，CPD-KL 为 1.11–3.14。状态和边的条件恢复总体稳定且较强，但严格完整 CPD 一致性仍然困难。使用独立生成的 GPT-5.5 参考结果时，趋势依旧；人工试验也支持结构具有较好的可恢复性，并显示出相近的概率解释。

Conclusion: PRISM-BN 为文本到参数化贝叶斯网络任务提供了规模化、受控且可分解的评测资源。研究表明，当前模型较擅长恢复条件节点状态和网络边结构，但在精确估计完整多父节点 CPD 方面仍存在明显不足。该基准能够将结构恢复能力与概率参数估计能力分开衡量，为后续神经符号 BN 抽取和训练研究提供基础。

Abstract: Probabilistic Graphical Models (PGMs), especially Bayesian Networks (BNs), expose directed structure and probabilistic parameters, making them natural symbolic targets for neurosymbolic AI. Yet training text-to-parameterized-BN systems requires paired text-to-BN resources unavailable at scale. We introduce PRISM-BN, a controlled corpus of 5054 BN-grounded descriptions paired with discrete reference BNs containing variables, states, directed edges, root priors, and full multi-parent CPDs across five domains. The instances are derived from 50 Wikipedia-seeded backbones, and their probabilities are internally constructed benchmark targets rather than externally validated causal estimates. PRISM-BN is built with PRISM, a marginal-first pipeline that elicits marginal and local joint distributions, analytically recovers normalized CPDs, and constructs locally reparameterized subgraphs. We define a benchmark with semantic node and state alignment, conditional structural scoring, and strict full-CPD evaluation. Across six LLM extractors, Node F1 ranges from 0.56 to 0.83, conditional Edge F1 from 0.90 to 0.97, and CPD-KL from 1.11 to 3.14. Conditional state and edge recovery remain consistently strong, whereas strict full-CPD agreement remains challenging. These trends persist with independently generated GPT-5.5 references, and a human pilot corroborates structural recoverability and similar probabilistic interpretations. PRISM-BN supports separate evaluation of structural recovery and probabilistic parameter estimation.

</details>


### [127] [CIBuzzBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Understanding of Chinese Internet Buzzwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21722)
*Yifan Wang,Junyu Lu,Qifan Wang,Shun Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Jiahao Liu,Zhijun Cao,Lingbin Bu,Fanliang Bu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CIBuzzBench，这是首个面向中文互联网流行语中英跨语言理解的基准数据集。数据集包含3,001条流行语及其英文释义、英文对应表达、类别和有害性标签，并设置含义解释、跨语言等价匹配和文化语境有害性检测三个任务。对多种先进专有及中文大语言模型的评测表明，模型在非字面含义理解、受选项扰动影响的等价表达匹配，以及有害性检测校准方面仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文互联网流行语具有强烈的文化和语用依赖，其含义常常并非字面意义。现有研究主要关注中文语境内的解释，对于大语言模型能否将这类文化知识迁移到英语并准确传达原意，研究仍较有限。此外，文化特定的谐音、委婉语、反讽和编码表达可能掩盖冒犯或有害内容，因此跨语言理解能力也是安全评测的重要组成部分。本文旨在系统评估大语言模型对中文互联网流行语的跨语言理解能力及其安全风险识别能力。

Method: 作者构建 CIBuzzBench 基准，收录3,001条中文互联网流行语，并为每条数据提供英文含义解释、英文等价表达、类别标签和有害性标签。在此基础上设计三个任务：1）Meaning Explanation，评估模型生成或理解英文释义的能力；2）Cross-lingual Equivalent Matching，评估中文流行语与英文对应表达的匹配能力，并通过选项扰动检验鲁棒性；3）Culturally Grounded Harmfulness Detection，评估模型识别受文化语境影响的有害表达的能力。实验覆盖具有代表性的先进专有大语言模型和中文大语言模型，并分别采用中文提示与英文提示进行评测。

Result: 实验结果显示，现有大语言模型在中文互联网流行语的跨语言理解方面仍面临显著困难。具体而言，模型在细粒度的非字面含义解释、对选项扰动具有鲁棒性的中英等价表达匹配，以及经过良好校准的有害性检测方面表现不足。结果表明，模型能力并非仅受语言转换影响，还受到文化背景、语用推理和安全判断的共同制约。

Conclusion: 中文互联网流行语代表了一类对多语言大语言模型具有挑战性的文化扎根语言现象。CIBuzzBench 为评估此类跨语言理解和安全能力提供了系统化工具。研究表明，当前模型尚未可靠掌握中文流行语的隐含文化语义，也难以稳定识别其中可能被隐蔽表达的有害内容。未来需要加强文化语境建模、非字面语义推理、跨语言等价表达判断和安全检测校准。

Abstract: Chinese social media has generated a vast and continually evolving lexicon of internet buzzwords whose meanings are often non-literal and deeply rooted in local cultural and pragmatic contexts. Existing research has primarily focused on interpreting these buzzwords within Chinese, leaving largely unexplored whether LLMs can transfer such culturally grounded knowledge across languages and accurately convey the intended meanings in English. This cross-lingual capability is also critical for safety, as harmful expressions may obscure their offensive content through culture-specific homophony, euphemism, irony, or coded language. In this paper, we investigate the ability of advanced LLMs to understand Chinese internet buzzwords across languages. To this end, we introduce CIBuzzBench, the first benchmark for cross-lingual Chinese-to-English understanding of Chinese internet buzzwords. CIBuzzBench comprises 3,001 Chinese internet buzzwords annotated with English meaning explanations, English equivalents, category labels, and harmfulness labels. Based on these annotations, we design three evaluation tasks: Meaning Explanation, Cross-lingual Equivalent Matching, and Culturally Grounded Harmfulness Detection. We evaluate representative state-of-the-art proprietary and Chinese LLMs under both English- and Chinese-prompting settings. Our results show that LLMs continue to struggle with the cross-lingual understanding of Chinese internet buzzwords, particularly in fine-grained non-literal interpretation, robust equivalent matching under option perturbations, and calibrated harmfulness detection. These findings highlight the persistent challenges posed by culturally grounded language phenomena for multilingual LLMs and safety-oriented evaluation. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/SuperYFan/CIBuzzBench.

</details>


### [128] [Per-Aetiology Contrastive Severity Embeddings with Phonological Pseudo-Labelling for Multilingual Dysarthric Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21789)
*Bernard Muller,Antonio Armando Ortiz Barrañón,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在统一的 HuBERT-base 对比学习框架、训练流程、数据登记和独立测试集下，比较混合病因模型与 CP、PD、ALS 三个病因专属模型。结果显示，病因专属模型在三类目标病因上均显著优于混合模型；在 CP 上加入伪标签数据后性能进一步提升。研究支持按病因设计标签空间，而非简单合并异质病因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语种构音障碍严重度评估系统通常局限于单一病因—语言组合，或将不同病因混合到同一标签空间中。后者隐含了一个未经充分验证的假设：不同病因导致的言语障碍模式可以被统一建模。本文旨在通过严格匹配的对照实验检验这一假设，并评估训练免标注的音系特征分析方法生成的序数伪标签是否能有效补充临床标注数据。

Method: 研究采用共享 HuBERT-base 骨干网络、相同训练方案、语料库登记和留出评估设置，构建四个对比学习模型：一个混合病因基线模型，以及分别针对脑瘫（CP）、帕金森病（PD）和肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）的三个病因专属模型。训练数据同时包含临床标注语音和由免训练音系分析方法生成的序数伪标签。评估采用说话人独立且经过数据泄漏过滤的留出子集，指标为宏平均 F1。数据覆盖每种病因对应的 3 至 7 种语言；此外，在 CP 实验中比较仅使用临床数据与加入 144 名 SAP、44 名 CDSD 伪标签说话人的模型。

Result: 病因专属模型在三类病因上均优于混合基线：CP 的宏平均 F1 为 0.829 对 0.676，相对提升 22.6%；PD 为 0.715 对 0.511，相对提升 40.0%；ALS 为 0.788 对 0.596，相对提升 32.3%。在 CP 上，加入伪标签数据后，宏平均 F1 从临床数据模型的 0.786 提升至 0.829，增加 4.3 个百分点。结果表明，病因特异性标签空间和适当的伪标签扩充都可能带来性能收益。

Conclusion: 研究支持在多语种构音障碍严重度建模中采用病因专属模型，而不是默认将异质病因合并到单一标签空间。伪标签可在临床标注有限时提供补充信息，但其校准质量和可靠性仍需进一步验证。作者同时强调，未来部署应关注伪标签校准、数据划分与泄漏控制，以及基于置信度阈值的安全使用。由于摘要未提供统计显著性检验、各病因样本量和跨数据集泛化结果，因此结论主要适用于当前数据与实验设置，尚不足以证明普遍优越性。

Abstract: Most multilingual dysarthria-severity systems either train on a single aetiology-language pair or pool heterogeneous aetiologies into one label space. We test that pooling assumption with four matched HuBERT-base contrastive embedding models under a shared backbone, training recipe, corpus registry and held-out evaluation: one mixed-aetiology baseline and three aetiology-specific models for cerebral palsy (CP), Parkinson's disease (PD) and amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS). Training combines clinically labelled speech with ordinal pseudo-labels from a training-free phonological profiling method [1], [2]. On speaker-disjoint, leakage-filtered held-out subsets, the per-aetiology models outperform the mixed baseline across all three target aetiologies: CP (macro F1 0.829 vs 0.676, +22.6 % relative), PD (0.715 vs 0.511, +40.0 %) and ALS (0.788 vs 0.596, +32.3 %). On CP, adding 144 SAP and 44 CDSD pseudo-labelled speakers lifts macro F1 from 0.786 to 0.829 over a clinical-only CP model (+4.3 percentage points). Training data span three to seven languages per aetiology. We position this as a controlled comparison of label-space design choices and discuss pseudo-label calibration, split hygiene, and confidence-thresholded deployment as important limitations for future work.

</details>


### [129] [RheoSampling: Resolving the One-Hot Dilemma in Stochastic Dynamic-Tree Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21827)
*Qiao Hu,Yepeng Weng,Bo Zhang,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RheoSampling，一种面向随机解码的动态树推测解码方法，通过解耦树构建与 token 验证所使用的概率，在保持无损采样的同时提升接受率与推理加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态树方法主要针对贪心解码设计，依赖确定性的 top-K 扩展和全局剪枝；在温度 T>0 的随机解码中，这种机制会将草稿分布压缩为近似 one-hot，导致接受率显著下降。静态树虽能保留随机采样，却缺乏上下文感知能力。根本原因是同一概率分布同时承担树结构构建和 token 验证两个相互冲突的任务。

Method: RheoSampling 将树构建与验证解耦：从草稿分布中采样一个 token，并将其加入确定性 top-K 候选位置；在树扩展和剪枝时，为该 token 使用代理概率，在验证阶段则保留其真实采样概率，从而兼顾上下文感知的动态 top-K 构建与随机采样。方法通过等价类分析压缩随机树空间并证明无损性；同时采用基于最优传输（OT）的验证策略和稀疏草稿机制，以降低实际计算开销并实现理论收益。

Result: 在多个大语言模型和基准上的实验表明，RheoSampling 相较现有先进动态树方法能够提高 token 接受率和整体推理加速比，并在随机解码场景下保持无损性。摘要未提供具体模型、数据集或数值提升幅度。

Conclusion: 本文解决了动态树推测解码在随机采样下接受率下降的关键问题。通过概率角色解耦，RheoSampling 首次将上下文感知的动态 top-K 树构建与随机采样结合起来，并以等价类分析建立无损保证。该框架也为随机树结构的理论分析和后续设计提供了通用思路。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting multiple tokens in parallel, with tree-based methods further improving efficiency through hierarchical structures. Dynamic-tree methods such as EAGLE-3 perform well under greedy decoding via deterministic top-K expansion and global pruning. However, in stochastic decoding (T>0), this mechanism collapses the draft distribution into one-hot probabilities, causing a severe drop in acceptance rate. This creates a dilemma: dynamic-tree methods sacrifice stochastic sampling to preserve context-aware topology, while static-tree methods preserve stochastic sampling with context-agnostic structures. The issue arises because the same probability distribution is used for two conflicting tasks: constructing the tree and verifying tokens. This coupling makes direct injection of randomness challenging due to the resulting stochastic process. We resolve this by decoupling these roles: RheoSampling assigns a token sampled from the draft distribution a proxy probability for tree expansion and pruning alongside its true sampling probability for verification. Specifically, we inject a sampled token among the deterministic top-K slots and treat it with different probabilities during construction and verification, making RheoSampling the first dynamic-tree method with both context-aware top-K construction and stochastic sampling while maintaining losslessness. We establish the lossless guarantee through an equivalence-class analysis that compresses the stochastic tree space into tractable classes. An OT-based verification strategy and a sparse draft mechanism ensure that theoretical gains translate into practical efficiency. Experiments across LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate improvements in acceptance rate and speedup over state-of-the-art dynamic tree methods. This framework may provide a template for analyzing stochastic tree structures.

</details>


### [130] [Reusing Latent Speech Representations for Query-Conditioned Topic Localization in Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21844)
*Steffen Freisinger,Philipp Seeberger,Thomas Ranzenberger,Tobias Bocklet,Korbinian Riedhammer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究查询条件下的主题定位：根据主题标题查询，在长篇转录文本中预测最能回答该主题的句子片段。方法是复用ASR编码器状态作为句子级语音表示，并与文本嵌入融合，从而在不增加独立音频编码器的情况下增强轻量级跨度定位器。两个公开数据集实验表明，该方法 consistently 优于纯文本基线，尤其在严格边界匹配指标下；跨数据集结果显示，收益在结构化或半结构化语音中最明显，而在自发语音中有限且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇转录文本输入成本高，且常包含与查询无关的上下文，导致下游自然语言处理系统难以高效定位相关内容。现有纯文本方法忽略了自动语音识别过程中蕴含的语音结构信息，因此本文希望以较低计算成本利用ASR表示，提升与主题标题相关的句子跨度定位能力。

Method: 任务定义为查询条件下的主题定位，即给定转录文本和主题标题，预测最相关的句子跨度。方法复用ASR编码器的隐藏状态，将其聚合或转换为句子级语音表示，并与文本嵌入进行融合，再输入轻量级跨度定位器。该设计避免运行独立音频编码器，同时引入语音侧信息。实验在两个公开数据集上进行，并通过跨数据集测试分析不同语音类型下的泛化表现。

Result: 融合ASR状态和文本表示的方法在两个数据集上均较纯文本基线取得稳定提升，且在严格边界匹配指标下提升尤为明显，说明该方法不仅能识别相关区域，也有助于更精确地确定跨度边界。跨数据集实验表明，结构化或半结构化语音中的收益最强；自发语音中的收益较小，并呈现混合或不稳定趋势。

Conclusion: 复用ASR编码器状态是一种计算高效的多模态增强策略，能够在不引入独立音频编码器的前提下改善查询条件下的主题跨度定位，尤其适用于结构清晰的语音内容。但其对自发语音的泛化能力仍有限，未来需要进一步处理口语表达、话题跳转和边界噪声等问题。

Abstract: Long transcripts are costly inputs for downstream NLP systems and often contain irrelevant context. We study query-conditioned topic localization: predicting the sentence span in a transcript that best addresses a topic-title query. To improve span localization, we reuse ASR encoder states as sentence-level representations and fuse them with textual embeddings. This lets lightweight span locators exploit speech information without running a separate audio encoder. Experiments on two public datasets show consistent gains over text-only baselines, especially under strict boundary-matching criteria. Cross-dataset experiments further indicate that the benefits are strongest for structured or semi-structured speech, while gains on spontaneous speech are limited and mixed.

</details>


### [131] [Do Personality-Tuned LLMs Make Better Social Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21857)
*Tim Krabbe,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究考察人格感知微调是否能提升小型开源大语言模型在社会模拟中的人格一致性、可控性与对话质量。结果显示，微调模型在不同人格角色扮演上并未显著优于基线；基线模型总体表现最佳，且评审者间一致性偏低限制了结论可信度。微调仅在Qwen模型上改善了语言多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能够生成高度拟人的社会互动行为，但其输出仍常表现出明显的“非人感”，尤其体现在人格表达不稳定、难以控制。现有仅依赖指令提示的方法可能不足以实现可靠的人格条件对话生成，因此研究者希望验证人格感知微调能否缩小这一差距，并提升社会模拟中角色行为的真实性与一致性。

Method: 研究者使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Ministral-8B-Instruct两种小型开源模型，基于融合人格标注社交媒体文本与对话的数据集进行人格导向微调，构建人格条件对话引擎。随后在多个社会互动场景中，将微调模型与各自基线模型进行比较。评估由三名独立的大语言模型评审者完成，主要考察人格保真度，并要求提供基于证据的行为解释；同时分析评审者间一致性以及生成文本的词汇特征。

Result: 微调模型在不同人格的角色扮演能力上没有优于对应的基线模型。由于三名评审者之间的一致性较低，相关结果的解释可信度受到限制。在文本质量方面，微调模型总体与基线相当；其中，Qwen系列微调模型的语言多样性有所提升。综合表现上，基线模型仍然最好。

Conclusion: 人格感知微调并未稳定提升人格角色扮演的准确性、连贯性或可控性，因此仅凭该方法难以消除大语言模型在社会模拟中的“非人感”。研究结果表明，未来应更加重视训练数据的质量、规模与领域匹配程度，并改进人格评估方法及评审一致性分析，以获得更可靠的结论。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used in social simulations for socially interactive agents and robots, offering more flexibility than rule-based systems. However, even though they mimic human behaviour very well, there is a persistent alienness to them. This work investigates whether personality-aware fine-tuning can reduce this gap by improving the consistency and controllability of personality-conditioned dialogue generation compared with instruction prompting alone. We fine-tune two small open-weight LLMs, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Ministral-8B-Instruct, using a corpus that combines personality-labelled social media posts and dialogues to create a personality-based dialogue engine for social simulation. The resulting models are evaluated across multiple social interaction scenarios using three independent LLM judges, which assess personality fidelity and provide evidence-based behavioral interpretations. We additionally quantify inter-rater agreement and lexical characteristics of the generated dialogue. Results indicate that fine-tuned models are not better at role-playing different personalities than their respective baseline models. However, low inter-rater agreement limits the confidence with which these results can be interpreted. Concerning the quality of generated texts, fine-tuned models are mostly comparable to the baselines, with fine-tuning improving the linguistic diversity of the Qwen models. While the results appear generally usable and the baseline models offer the best overall performance, future studies should place greater emphasis on the quality and domain alignment of training data for accurate personality role-playing.

</details>


### [132] [TrialAtlas: Multi-Agent Research Organization for Clinical Trial Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21859)
*Jiacheng Lin,Zifeng Wang,Zheng Chen,Erick Scott,Ziwei Yang,Fanyang Yu,Sheng Zhong,Jimeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrialAtlas：一种记忆增强的多智能体临床开发规划系统，通过协调文献综述、竞品试验情报、监管先例分析和综合推理代理，辅助试验设计缺陷识别、设计改进建议及技术与监管成功概率预测。基于291份FDA Complete Response Letters构建的TrialAtlasBench评测显示，该系统在缺陷检测和成功预测任务上均优于基线，并获得较高的专家认可度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床开发失败率极高，进入临床开发的药物约90%最终失败，造成巨额资金和时间损失。现有临床开发规划与技术/监管成功概率评估依赖跨学科专家协作，涉及异构证据的搜集、整合和判断，过程劳动密集且具有主观性。因此，研究旨在构建能够模拟专家协作、利用历史开发经验并提升决策一致性与准确性的智能系统。

Method: 研究提出TrialAtlas，一种记忆增强的多智能体研究组织。系统由多个专业代理协同完成文献综合、竞争性临床试验分析、监管先例分析，以及围绕试验设计和开发风险的综合推理；同时利用历史临床试验和监管结果（包括既往新药申请）作为经验记忆。研究还构建TrialAtlasBench，基于291份FDA Complete Response Letters，覆盖三项任务：识别试验设计缺陷、提出可执行的设计改进建议、预测技术与监管成功。

Result: 在缺陷检测任务中，TrialAtlas的F1为50.0%，比最强基线高6.1个百分点。在技术与监管成功预测任务中，平衡准确率为85.3%，F1为84.7%；相较最佳基线，平衡准确率提升6.7个百分点，Cohen’s kappa提升12.0个百分点。专家评估中，TrialAtlas生成的问题和担忧有86.4%被判定为有效，高于OpenAI DeepResearch的83.1%和Gemini DeepResearch的59.3%。

Conclusion: TrialAtlas表明，结合多智能体协作与历史监管经验记忆，能够有效支持临床开发规划，并在试验缺陷识别、设计优化和成功概率预测方面优于现有基线。该研究的主要贡献还包括建立了面向真实监管场景的TrialAtlasBench。不过，摘要未说明数据划分、外部验证、不同代理模块的独立贡献及系统在真实决策流程中的前瞻性效果，因此其临床应用价值仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Nearly 90% of drugs entering clinical development ultimately fail, despite billions of dollars in investment.
