Pharmaceutical companies therefore rely on clinical development planning (CDP) and probability of technical and regulatory success assessment to anticipate development risks, yet these decisions remain labor-intensive and subjective, requiring experts across clinical science, statistics, regulatory affairs, and competitive intelligence to jointly acquire, synthesize, and reason over heterogeneous evidence. Here, we introduce TrialAtlas, a memory-augmented multi-agent research organization for CDP that mirrors this collaborative process by coordinating specialized agents for literature synthesis, competitive trial intelligence, regulatory precedent analysis, and integrated reasoning over trial design and development risk. TrialAtlas further learns from historical clinical trials and regulatory outcomes, including prior New Drug Applications (NDAs), to ground its decisions in accumulated development experience. To evaluate these capabilities in an authentic regulatory setting, we introduce TrialAtlasBench, constructed from 291 FDA Complete Response Letters and spanning three practical tasks: detecting trial design deficiencies, recommending actionable design improvements, and predicting technical and regulatory success. TrialAtlas achieves an F1 score of 50.0% for deficiency detection, outperforming the strongest baseline by 6.1 points, and reaches 85.3% balanced accuracy and 84.7% F1 for prediction of technical and regulatory success, improving over the best baselines by 6.7 points in balanced accuracy and 12.0 points in Cohen's kappa. In expert evaluation, 86.4% of TrialAtlas-generated concerns were judged valid, compared with 83.1% for OpenAI DeepResearch and 59.3% for Gemini DeepResearch.

</details>


### [133] [NemotronLabs VoiceChat: An Open Full-duplex Speech-to-Speech Model with Tool Calling Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21967)
*Jagadeesh Balam,Travis Bartley,Edresson Casanova,Sanjay Chauhan,Chen Chen,Zhehuai Chen,Zijia Chen,Francesco Ciannella,Slyne Deng,Mikyas Desta,Harishchandra Dubey,Slim Essid,Nourchene Ferchichi,Boris Ginsburg,Mariana Graterol Fuenmayor,Negar Habibi,Kevin Hu,Anand Joseph,Viraj Karandikar,Myungjong Kim,Viacheslav Klimkov,Seelan Lakshmi Narasimhan,Lily Lee,Jason Li,Eileen Long,Ameya Mahabaleshwarkar,Aditya Malte,Adi Margolin,Sasha Meister,Valentin Mendelev,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Ankita Pasad,Yifan Peng,Elena Rastorgueva,Jayda Ritchie,Jason Roche,Nikhil Srihari,Yuanhang Su,Yoshi Suhara,Viet Anh Trinh,Jinhan Wang,Piotr Zelasko,Hui Wang,Puhui Meng,Chaosen Zhang,Yunsheng Liu,Shawn Wang,Wenjing Li,Zhonglei He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 NemotronLabs VoiceChat：一种开放权重、全双工、端到端语音到语音模型，原生支持工具调用。模型将流式语音编码器、仅解码器语言模型、并行文本与结构化函数调用输出、增量式 RNN-T 转写分支及流式 TTS 解码器整合在统一架构中，实现实时聆听、转写、推理、调用工具和语音回复。在多个基准上，模型展现出较强的打断处理、恢复响应和工具选择能力，但工具参数准确率与端到端执行能力仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音助手通常将语音识别、语言理解、工具调用和语音合成为多个串联系统，难以同时实现自然的全双工交互、低延迟响应和可靠的工具使用。本文旨在构建一个开放的统一语音到语音模型，使系统能够在用户讲话、插话或提供反馈时持续进行实时处理，并兼顾通用语言能力与外部工具调用能力。

Method: 模型采用统一流式架构：使用流式语音编码器接收输入，使用仅解码器语言模型进行推理；通过并行的专用输出流分别生成代理文本和结构化函数调用；利用辅助 RNN-T 分支进行增量式用户语音转写；通过流式 TTS 解码器实时生成语音输出。该设计支持聆听、转写、推理、工具调用和语音回复的并行或连续执行，并保持自然对话所需的时间行为。

Result: 在 Full-Duplex-Bench 1.0 上，模型在评测的开放权重系统中实现最低的暂停处理接管率，用户打断后的接管率达到 100%，打断后响应质量评分为 4.33/5。在 Full-Duplex-Bench 1.5 上，模型在 93% 的用户 backchannel 情况下能够恢复响应。在 VoiceBench 上取得 55.1 的归一化平均分。在 Full-Duplex-Bench 3.0 上，工具选择 F1 达到 82.5%，但工具参数准确率和端到端工具执行能力仍需改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明，全双工交互、语音识别与生成、通用语言推理以及外部工具调用可以整合到单一的开放语音到语音模型中，同时保持实时对话行为。模型在打断处理和工具选择方面表现突出，但其工具调用链条中的参数生成与实际执行可靠性仍是后续优化重点。

Abstract: We introduce NemotronLabs VoiceChat, an open full-duplex speech-to-speech model with native tool-calling capabilities. NemotronLabs VoiceChat combines a streaming speech encoder and decoder-only language model with parallel specialized output streams for agent text and structured function calls, an auxiliary RNN-T branch for incremental user transcription, and a streaming TTS decoder. This design enables the model to listen, transcribe, reason, invoke tools, and speak within a unified streaming architecture while preserving the temporal behavior required for natural conversation. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.0, NemotronLabs VoiceChat achieves the lowest pause-handling takeover rates among evaluated open-weight systems, 100\% takeover following user interruptions, and a 4.33/5 post-interruption response-quality score. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.5, it resumes its response after user backchannels in 93\% of cases. NemotronLabs VoiceChat obtains a 55.1 normalized average on VoiceBench and, on Full-Duplex-Bench 3.0 (FDB 3.0), achieves 82.5\% tool-selection F1, while argument accuracy and end-to-end tool execution remain areas for improvement. These results demonstrate that full-duplex interaction, speech recognition and generation, general language capabilities, and external tool use can be integrated in a single open speech-to-speech model without sacrificing real-time conversational behavior.

</details>


### [134] [Moral Entropy: Auditing Bias and Uncertainty in Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21992)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Moral Entropy，一个用贝叶斯后验建模道德标签不确定性的框架，将标注者分歧区分为不可约的道德内容歧义（偶然不确定性）与由标注不足或噪声造成的认识不确定性（认知不确定性），并据此审计常见标签聚合规则。实验表明，any-annotator 规则产生大量假阳性，而多数票和双票规则又遗漏大量真实阳性，说明简单共识规则存在系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算伦理研究通常将标注者对道德内容的分歧视为噪声，并通过多数票或 any-annotator 规则压缩为单一标签。这种做法无法保留道德判断本身的歧义，也无法区分真实分歧与标注信息不足导致的不确定性，因此可能造成未被报告的系统性偏差。本文旨在对不确定性进行显式建模，并用校准后的真实标签分布评估传统聚合规则。

Method: 提出 Moral Entropy 贝叶斯框架，对真实标签维护完整后验分布，并将总熵分解为偶然不确定性（关于道德内容的不可约分歧）和认知不确定性（由标注不足或噪声引起）。框架使用交叉熵、KL 散度、Brier 分数和期望校准误差等指标，将启发式共识规则与校准后的后验真值进行审计。实验覆盖三个语料库和十五个话语领域，并比较 any-annotator、多数票及双票规则。

Result: 审计发现，any-annotator 规则与校准后验在约30%的样本上不一致；总体上这些错误几乎全部是假阳性。在 MFTC 基础层级，平均假阳性率和假阴性率分别为19.9%和38.9%，表明错误类型会随分析层级发生反转。更严格的多数票和双票规则则遗漏63%—83%的真实阳性。

Conclusion: 道德标注分歧不应被简单视为噪声或直接通过投票消除，而应作为可学习、可校准的不确定性进行建模。Moral Entropy 揭示了不同聚合规则在假阳性和假阴性之间的显著权衡，并为评估计算伦理数据集的标签质量、模型校准性与决策可靠性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Most work in computational ethics treats annotator disagreement on moral content as noise to be voted away, collapsed into majority vote or the more permissive any-annotator rule the moment a single annotator flags an item. We argue this uncertainty should instead be modeled and learned from.
  We introduce Moral Entropy, a Bayesian framework that keeps a full posterior over the true label and decomposes its entropy into aleatoric uncertainty (irreducible disagreement about the moral content) and epistemic uncertainty (from insufficient or noisy annotation) -- and lets any heuristic consensus rule be audited against a calibrated ground truth via entropy methods such as cross-entropy/KL, Brier score, and expected calibration error.
  Across three corpora and fifteen discourse domains, auditing the standard aggregation rules against this posterior reveals bias that no current pipeline reports: the any-annotator rule disagrees with the calibrated posterior on roughly 30% of items -- pooled, almost entirely false positives, though the errors invert at the foundation level (19.9%/38.9% mean FPR/FNR on MFTC) -- while the stricter majority and two-vote rules miss 63-83% of true positives.

</details>


### [135] [RecreationWorld: Scalable and Verifiable Environments for Hybrid Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22000)
*Shuai Bai,Jiayong Deng,Yikun Fu,Chang Gao,Xuhao Hu,Mianqiu Huang,Yizhen Jiang,Yuheng Jing,Dehui Kong,Keliang Li,Ning Li,Wanli Li,Dayiheng Liu,Dunjie Lu,Changwei Luo,Que Shen,Zheyuan Wang,Zijian Wang,Jie Wu,Gao Wu,Zhihui Xie,Rui Xie,Haiyang Xu,An Yang,Jiakang Yuan,Yanming Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Xi Zhang,Zhenru Zhang,Zhuo Zhen,Mingkang Zhu,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RecreationWorld 与 RecreationBench，用于训练和评估能够交替执行图形界面操作、代码开发、运行及视觉验证的混合计算机使用智能体。实验表明，基于再现任务训练的模型可迁移到多种编码与混合计算机使用基准，但当前智能体更擅长复现静态界面，对交互逻辑和计算结果的复现仍较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体主要沿两条相对独立的路线发展：一类专注于图形界面交互，另一类通过代码和命令行进行软件开发。然而真实数字工作通常要求二者交替结合，而非简单串联。现有研究缺少能够衡量智能体是否真正理解参考软件行为、能否实现并验证完整应用的统一环境和基准。因此，本文旨在构建可复现的混合任务框架，推动智能体从界面模仿走向功能级软件再现。

Method: 作者提出 RecreationWorld，一个覆盖 Ubuntu、macOS、Windows、Android 和 Web 五个平台的五平台框架。任务形式是：给定一个正在运行的参考应用，智能体自主探索其行为，使用代码和命令行构建实现，并运行应用、通过图形界面进行视觉验证；不规定固定工作流。框架提供统一测试工具、原生 GUI 控制和编程工具，并将参考应用作为隐藏行为测试的执行 oracle，以产生基于实际执行的奖励。作者利用高质量开源应用扩展轨迹生成规模，并基于这些轨迹训练模型。评估方面，作者提出 RecreationBench，包含 250 个跨领域、跨平台任务，使用参考应用驱动的程序化断言和视觉断言，覆盖不同交互深度；所有测试均在参考应用上验证，并由人工审查后固定，用于自动评分。

Result: 训练于 RecreationWorld 轨迹的模型在五个分布外编码和混合计算机使用基准上均有提升，并且更频繁地验证渲染结果，说明再现任务训练具有超越训练任务本身的迁移能力。在 RecreationBench 上，GPT-6 Astra 的总体得分为 58.1%，但仅有 2.8% 的任务通过全部程序化测试。智能体对静态界面结构的复现明显优于对交互行为和计算结果的复现；生成的应用通常比参考应用更小、更单体化。

Conclusion: 本文证明，将 GUI 操作、软件实现、运行测试和视觉验证整合到统一的再现任务中，有助于训练更具迁移性的混合计算机使用智能体。RecreationWorld 和 RecreationBench 为该方向提供了跨平台、可执行、可自动评分的研究基础。不过，当前系统距离可靠复现真实软件仍有明显差距，主要瓶颈包括深层交互逻辑、状态依赖行为、计算输出正确性以及复杂软件架构的重建。作者发布基准、环境和测试套件，以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have advanced along two separate lines: graphical interaction and software development through code and the command line. Real digital work requires both, interleaved rather than stacked end to end. We study hybrid CUAs that autonomously decide when to explore an interface, implement software, and run and visually verify their artifacts. We introduce RecreationWorld, a five-platform framework built around recreation: given a running reference, an agent must discover its behavior and build a faithful implementation with no prescribed workflow. RecreationWorld provides reproducible environments on Ubuntu, macOS, Windows, Android, and Web, plus a unified harness with native GUI control and coding tools. The running reference serves as an oracle for hidden behavioral tests, providing execution-grounded rewards. We scale trajectory generation with high-quality open-source applications. Models trained on these trajectories improve across five out-of-distribution coding and hybrid computer-use benchmarks and more frequently verify their rendered outputs, providing evidence of transfer beyond recreation. For held-out evaluation, we introduce RecreationBench, comprising 250 diverse tasks across domains and platforms. Reference-grounded programmatic and visual assertions cover action-conditioned outcomes at multiple interaction depths; each is validated on the reference and by human reviewers before the suite is frozen for automatic scoring. GPT-6 Astra leads at 58.1% overall, but passes all programmatic tests on just 2.8% of tasks. Agents reproduce static interface structure more reliably than interactions and computed outputs, while generated applications remain smaller and more monolithic than their references. We release the benchmark, environments, and test suites.

</details>


### [136] [DiaVLo: Diagnosing Behaviours of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22008)
*Lorenzo Corti,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiaVLo 是一个用于诊断视觉语言模型（VLM）行为的框架，通过人工策划与模型生成能力构建期望行为和实际行为规范，并利用因果估计识别影响行为的关键概念。实验表明，该框架能发现模型的对齐与失配行为，并解释模型如何感知、组织和优先处理概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型由多个子组件构成，需要在组件之间正确存储和传递信息。仅评估整体性能不足以确认模型是否表现出期望行为，也难以发现潜在的有害或失配行为。由于现有针对VLM行为识别的方法较少，作者提出需要一种能够系统描述、比较并解释VLM行为的诊断框架。

Method: 提出DiaVLo诊断框架。首先结合人工策划与VLM的生成能力，构建关于期望行为和实际观察行为的行为规范或标签；然后将这些行为描述用于诊断模型表现、揭示行为与性能之间的关系。框架进一步采用因果估计，识别对VLM行为具有最大影响力的概念，从而分析模型如何感知、组织和优先处理信息。作者在多个开源VLM上，分别针对分类和生成任务进行评估。

Result: DiaVLo生成的行为标签与模型性能呈相关性，并能为测得的性能提供语境解释。实验还发现了明显符合预期的行为和失配行为，以及不同VLM在概念感知、组织和优先级排序方面的模式。因果分析能够进一步定位对模型行为最具影响力的概念。

Conclusion: DiaVLo为识别和解释视觉语言模型行为提供了一种可行的诊断方法，不仅能够判断模型是否达到预期，还能揭示潜在失配及其概念层面的驱动因素。该框架可作为VLM可靠部署前进行行为验证和模型分析的工具，但摘要未说明其因果估计的具体假设、标注成本及跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on storing and transferring appropriate information across their sub-components. Verifying that the VLMs exhibit desired behaviours, while avoiding harmful ones, is central to their reliable deployment. Yet, methods that identify VLM behaviours remain scarce. We present DiaVLo, a diagnostic framework that leverages human curation and VLMs' generation capabilities to construct specifications of desired and observed VLM behaviours, surfacing potential misalignments. Beyond this, DiaVLo also provides causal estimates to identify the most influential concepts steering VLM behaviours. We evaluate DiaVLo on several open-source VLMs under both classification and generation conditions. Our experiments show that DiaVLo produces behaviour labels that correlate with model performance and provide context for measured performance. DiaVLo surfaced behaviours that are clearly aligned and misaligned, alongside patterns in how VLMs perceive, organise, and prioritise concepts.

</details>


### [137] [QuranicMMLU: A Cognitively-Aware Benchmark for Evaluating Generative AI Solutions on Quranic Linguistic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22038)
*Rawan El Ghali,Umm Kulsoom,Anas Madkoor,Dima Faris Alsaudi,Roaa Abdelmagid,Roaa Ibrahim,Raghad Mousa,Hamza Aljaji,Abdullah Khanafer,Abdallah Alkanani,Salah Feras Alali,Rawan Khaled Mohamed,Ehsaneddin Asgari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍一个用于评估生成式人工智能在特定宗教经典阿拉伯语领域表现的基准数据集与评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相关基准主要关注通用问答和语义检索，缺乏对音系、形态、句法、语义、语用等具体语言能力，以及认知层级和文本难度的系统考察。

Method: 构建五大语言学维度及31个细分类别，按认知层级和文本困惑度生成题目；采用开放问答与选择题两种形式，并结合自动评审和人工复核；随后对12个系统进行基准测试。

Result: 数据集包含980道经人工复核的问题。专业化模型表现最佳。所有系统在选择题上的平均准确率为84%，而开放式回答质量平均为60%；两种任务的系统排名具有较高一致性，Kendall's tau为0.73，但选择题会掩盖开放式回答中暴露的错误。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了一个语言学导向、分层且较严格的评测框架，有助于更细致地分析生成式人工智能在该领域的语言理解与生成能力。

Abstract: We introduce QuranicMMLU, a benchmark for evaluating generative AI on Quranic Arabic across multiple dimensions of linguistic complexity. Existing Quranic benchmarks center on general question answering and semantic retrieval, without probing specific linguistic competencies or stratifying by cognitive demand and verse difficulty. We construct a five-pillar Quranic taxonomy spanning Phonology, Morphology, Syntax, Semantics, and Pragmatics, with 31 leaves covering phenomena from tajwīd and root-and-pattern morphology to occasions of revelation and inter-surah coherence. For each leaf we generate questions stratified by Bloom's cognitive level and verse perplexity, then have LLM as a judge to independently answer and score every item and route the annotations to manual review. The resulting dataset comprises 980 human-reviewed questions, each issued in both open-ended and multiple-choice form. We benchmark 12 systems on these items and find that the Islamic-specialized model leads, yet every system scores higher on multiple-choice accuracy (average 84%) than open-ended answer quality (average 60%): the two rankings agree closely (Kendall's τ=0.73), but multiple-choice scoring hides failures that surface only once answer choices are removed. QuranicMMLU thus offers a rigorous, linguistically grounded framework for evaluating Arabic NLP in the Quranic domain.

</details>


### [138] [An Interpretable Memory Decision Controller for LLM Agents Based on Three-Signal Complementarity: Decoupling Confidence and Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22043)
*Yiming Zhang,Jinghong Zhang,Haoran Zhao,Yiren Ma,Chunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆决策层（MDL），在检索与生成之间判断检索记忆是否可信，以降低冲突记忆导致的幻觉；该方法无需训练参数，推理开销极低，并在多种模型和数据集上显著降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型记忆系统主要关注记忆检索效率，却较少判断检索结果是否值得信任。当记忆库包含相互冲突的观点时，标准检索增强生成会不加区分地注入记忆，可能放大模型幻觉。论文因此关注如何对检索记忆进行可靠性评估，并在高风险情况下避免盲目使用不可信记忆。

Method: 论文提出位于检索阶段和生成阶段之间的零参数记忆决策控制器 MDL。其核心是三信号互补编码器，融合相关性、可靠性和任务风险，并通过基于 QR 分解的正交子空间投影及元工作记忆信号，构造可解释的记忆信任度表示。在此基础上，MDL 将置信度与一致性显式解耦，引入风险反转机制和显式弃答机制，从而决定接受、抑制或拒绝检索到的记忆。整个控制器仅使用几何运算，不依赖训练参数。

Result: 在多种主流大语言模型和多个开源数据集上的实验表明，MDL 在一般冲突记忆场景下将幻觉率降低约 56.04%；在高风险场景下，幻觉率接近于零。其单次决策额外耗时约 0.14 毫秒，约为前置向量检索步骤速度的 50 倍，并比调用大语言模型进行自评估快四到五个数量级。

Conclusion: MDL 通过在记忆注入前增加轻量、可解释且白盒化的信任决策，有效缓解了冲突记忆引发的幻觉问题。结果说明，记忆系统不仅需要高效检索，还需要可靠的记忆采纳与拒绝机制。该方法兼具较好的风险控制能力、可解释性和部署效率，但其性能仍可能依赖信号构造方式、风险定义及冲突记忆数据的代表性。

Abstract: Memory systems for large language models have focused predominantly on efficient retrieval, whereas the decision of whether retrieved memories should be trusted has received comparatively little attention. When the memory store contains conflicting positions, standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) blindly injects memories and amplifies hallucinations: in models susceptible to memory injection, the RAG hallucination rate under conflicting memories is markedly higher than that of a memory-free baseline. Inspired by memory signaling mechanisms in the prefrontal cortex, we propose the Memory Decision Layer (MDL), a zero-parameter memory decision controller situated between the retrieval and generation stages. Its core is a three-signal complementary encoder that fuses relevance, reliability, and task risk through QR-based orthogonal subspace projection and a meta-working-memory signal into an interpretable decision representation that quantifies the trustworthiness of retrieved memories. Building on this encoder, MDL explicitly decouples confidence from consistency and introduces risk inversion and explicit abstention. Evaluations on mainstream large language models and multiple open-source datasets show that MDL reduces the hallucination rate under conflicting memories by about 56.04% in general scenarios and approaches zero hallucination in high-risk scenarios. The controller is fully white-box: it relies purely on geometric operations, requires no trained parameters, and adds only about 0.14 ms per decision -- roughly 50x faster than the embedding-retrieval step that precedes it and four to five orders of magnitude faster than an LLM self-evaluation call.

</details>


### [139] [Cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in occupational accident narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22081)
*Aho Yapi,Pierre Latouche,Arnaud Guillin,Yan Bailly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究职业事故叙述中事故过程角色分类器的跨行业泛化能力。研究者仅使用建筑业数据训练模型，并在冶金、化学—塑料行业及独立企业语料上进行零样本式跨域评估。结果表明，任务特定适配显著优于冻结预训练表示，最佳方法在三个目标语料上的平均平衡准确率达到85.6%—85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业事故叙述包含工作情境、不利条件、事故事件及后果等关键信息，若能自动结构化，可支持大规模事故分析和职业风险预防。然而，不同行业和组织在术语与写作风格上差异明显，导致在单一行业训练的自动编码系统可能难以迁移到其他领域。因此，本文旨在评估事故过程角色分类在跨行业场景中的泛化能力，并比较不同文本表示与适配策略的迁移效果。

Method: 研究者构建了专家标注语料库，将事故叙述切分为事实单元，并划分为四类角色：工作情境（A0）、明确报告的不利条件（A1）、事故事件或偏离（B）以及报告的后果（C）。模型和策略仅在建筑业的6,040篇叙述、共42,244个事实单元上开发和选择，随后在未见过的冶金行业、化学—塑料行业语料，以及独立采集的企业语料上进行评估，且不进行重新训练或目标域调参。比较方法包括冻结的预训练表示、任务特定微调，以及监督式表示学习策略；采用多次重复训练和跨目标语料的平均平衡准确率评价性能。

Result: 任务特定适配在跨域迁移中持续优于冻结预训练表示。表现最好的三种任务适配策略在三个目标语料上的平均平衡准确率为85.6%—85.8%，说明模型即使仅在建筑业数据上训练，也能够较稳定地迁移至其他行业和独立企业语料。

Conclusion: 研究证明，结合任务特定表示适配的事故过程角色分类器具有较强的跨行业迁移能力。该结果支持开发可迁移的辅助编码系统，以持续结构化异质化职业事故叙述，并为专家复核、跨行业比较和职业风险预防分析提供支持。

Abstract: Occupational accident narratives contain valuable information about work situations, unfavourable conditions, accident events, and their consequences. Automatically structuring these narratives can facilitate large-scale accident analysis and support occupational risk prevention. However, the terminology and writing styles used to describe accidents vary considerably across sectors and organisations, raising questions about the ability of automated coding systems to generalize beyond their training domain. In this paper, we evaluate the cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in French occupational accident narratives. We construct an expert-annotated corpus in which factual units are classified into four roles: work situation (A0), explicitly reported unfavourable condition (A1), accident event or deviation (B), and reported consequence (C). The role classifiers are developed and selected exclusively on 42,244 factual units extracted from 6,040 construction-sector narratives and are then evaluated on unseen corpora from the metallurgy and chemistry--plastics sectors, as well as on an independently collected company corpus, without retraining or target-domain tuning of the role classifier. We compare frozen pretrained representations with task-specific fine-tuning and supervised representation-learning strategies. The results show that task-specific adaptation consistently improves cross-domain transfer over frozen representations. Across repeated training runs, the three leading task-adapted strategies achieved average balanced accuracies between 85.6% and 85.8% across the three target corpora. These findings support the development of transferable assisted-coding systems capable of consistently structuring heterogeneous occupational accident narratives for expert review and cross-sector prevention analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Task-Oriented Quantization for Quadratic Scheduling: Centroid Water-Filling and Power-Diagram Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20882)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文区分了任务导向量化中的两类问题：无约束且最优动作位于内部时，可先量化确定性预言机动作；预算约束二次调度时，则应量化负载并对质心执行注水，而不能直接量化注水后的动作。论文给出了前者的近似保证与后者的精确最优条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务导向量化方法可能将“量化预言机动作”这一策略直接推广到预算约束调度问题，但预算约束会使最优动作满足变分不等式，并产生注水过程中的活跃集切换。因此，需要明确不同结构下量化策略是否仍然最优，并刻画相应的最优性条件、近似性能和率失真关系。

Method: 针对无约束内部预言机和光滑强凹效用，使用向量 Lloyd–Max 量化最小化均方误差替代目标，并建立其与最优 K 级任务量化器之间的 β/α 近似关系；在各向同性二次损失下证明该约化是精确的，并通过两个普通率–失真函数对任务率–失真函数进行夹逼。针对预算约束二次调度，基于预言机满足的变分不等式推导精确的 Lloyd 型最优性条件，证明每个量化单元的最优动作等于对该单元条件均值负载执行注水，并将最优编码区域表示为仿射幂图单元。

Result: 无约束情形下，量化预言机动作具有可证明的 β/α 近似性能；对于各向同性二次损失，性能约化完全精确，任务率–失真函数可由两个普通率–失真函数上下界定。预算约束情形下，直接量化注水动作通常并非最优；最优策略是量化负载，再对每个量化单元的条件均值负载进行注水。相应编码器的分区具有仿射幂图结构。当一个量化单元跨越注水活跃集边界时，两种策略的差异尤其明显。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：量化对象必须与问题的几何结构匹配。对无约束内部预言机，量化预言机动作是有效且具有理论保证的；对预算约束调度，正确做法是“量化负载—对负载质心注水”，而非“量化已注水动作”。活跃集边界是导致后者失效的关键因素。

Abstract: We distinguish two regimes in task-oriented quantization with a known deterministic oracle action. For an unconstrained interior oracle and a smooth strongly concave utility, quantizing the oracle action by vector Lloyd-Max minimizes a mean-squared-error surrogate and achieves a $β/α$ approximation to the optimal $K$-level task quantizer. The reduction is exact for isotropic quadratic loss, and the corresponding task rate--distortion function is bracketed by two ordinary rate--distortion functions. Budget-constrained quadratic scheduling is different: the oracle satisfies a variational inequality, so quantizing water-filled actions is not generally optimal. We derive the exact Lloyd-type conditions for this case. The optimal action for a quantizer cell is water-filling evaluated at the cell's conditional-mean load, and the optimal encoder partitions load space into affine power-diagram cells. Thus the correct prescription is to quantize the load and water-fill its centroid. The distinction is material whenever a cell crosses water-filling active-set boundaries.

</details>


### [141] [SpaceDiffusion: Over-the-Orbit Diffusion for Space Generate-and-Forward Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20899)
*Jianhao Huang,Zhanwei Wang,Khaled B. Letaief,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaceDiffusion，一种利用卫星在轨生成式人工智能进行图像重构与转发的Generate-and-Forward通信框架。该方法将信道失真校正项融入DDIM更新，使单一预训练扩散模型能够适应不同丢包和压缩失真；同时提出误差分析、扩散步激活阈值和能量感知提前退出策略。实验表明，相较带重传的Decode-and-Forward方案，SpaceDiffusion可降低端到端时延，并在目标感知质量下节省约15 dB上行发射功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G卫星通信需要为地面设备提供广域、泛在连接，但卫星上行受到终端发射功率低、天线孔径有限等因素限制，导致数据速率低和数据包错误率高。传统Decode-and-Forward中继通常依赖重传来恢复错误数据，会增加时延和能耗。因此，论文探索让卫星利用在轨生成式人工智能直接重构受损信息，以缓解上行链路瓶颈。

Method: 提出Generate-and-Forward通信范式及SpaceDiffusion图像传输框架。首先，将压缩和丢失图像token的恢复建模为逆问题；其次，在标准DDIM去噪更新中直接加入信道失真校正项，使预训练扩散模型无需重新训练即可动态适应不同丢包模式和压缩失真；然后，分析逐步token重构误差，推导扩散步激活阈值，用于判断何时扩散重构优于传统DF中继；最后，设计能量感知的early-exit策略，以降低在轨部署的计算和能耗开销。

Result: 实验结果显示，SpaceDiffusion相较采用重传协议的DF方案具有更低的端到端时延，并且在达到目标感知质量时可节省约15 dB的上行发射功率。理论分析还提供了扩散步激活阈值，可用于预测其相对DF方案的性能优势。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在卫星侧部署生成式扩散模型能够将部分信道纠错与语义重构功能前移至在轨节点，从而有效缓解卫星上行的功率和可靠性瓶颈。SpaceDiffusion在降低时延和发射功率方面具有潜力，但其实际应用仍取决于卫星计算资源、扩散推理复杂度以及模型重构质量的稳定性。

Abstract: Satellite communications are an essential component of sixth-generation (6G) mobile networks, which provide ubiquitous connectivity for global services. However, the satellite uplink remains a critical bottleneck for ground devices: their limited transmit power and antenna apertures result in low data rates and high packet errors. To overcome this bottleneck, this paper advocates a novel relaying paradigm termed generate-and-forward (GF) communications, where satellites exploit on-orbit generative artificial intelligence (AI) to robustly reconstruct corrupted data prior to forwarding. Specifically, we propose SpaceDiffusion, an over-the-orbit diffusion framework for satellite-assisted image transmission. The core of this framework is a channel-distortion-aware diffusion theory developed using the following approach. By formulating the recovery of compressed and lost image tokens as an inverse problem, this theory incorporates a channel-distortion correction term directly into the conventional denoising diffusion implicit model (DDIM) update. As a result, this design enables a single pretrained diffusion model to adapt dynamically to varying packet-loss patterns and compression distortions without retraining. Furthermore, we analytically characterize the progressive token-reconstruction error and derive a diffusion-step activation threshold that predicts when SpaceDiffusion is expected to outperform conventional decode-and-forward (DF) relaying. Building on these theoretical insights, we further develop an energy-aware early-exit policy to efficiently deploy SpaceDiffusion in orbit. Experimental results demonstrate that SpaceDiffusion achieves lower end-to-end latency compared to DF scheme with retransmission protocol and saves approximately 15 dB of uplink transmit power at a target perceptual quality.

</details>


### [142] [Learning-based near- versus far-field boundaries for ultra-massive MIMO communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21055)
*Simon Tarboush,Nour Kouzayha,Hadi Sarieddeen,Tareq Y. Al-Naffouri,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需信道状态信息、完全无监督的近场/远场传播区域识别方法，利用超大规模MIMO阵列子阵列间的空间接收功率变化提取物理启发特征，并通过OPTICS聚类判断传播区域。仿真表明，该方法在不同系统配置和信噪比下均能准确识别传播区域，且结果与理论边界一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超大规模MIMO（UM-MIMO）系统中，近场与远场传播具有不同的信号处理机制，直接影响波束成形和信道估计。若无法准确判定通信所处的传播区域，可能导致算法选择不当、系统效率下降。现有方法通常依赖信道状态信息或已知距离，因此论文旨在设计一种仅基于接收信号测量、无需距离先验和信道状态信息的传播区域识别方法。

Method: 方法包括两个阶段：首先，利用UM阵列不同子阵列之间的空间接收信号功率变化，构造具有物理意义的特征，以反映近场和远场传播在阵列上的差异；其次，将这些特征输入OPTICS（基于对象排序识别聚类结构）算法，通过完全无监督聚类推断通信属于近场还是远场。该方法在区域识别之后还可为通信距离估计提供基础。

Result: 在多种系统配置和不同信噪比条件下进行的仿真结果显示，所提方法能够较准确地区分近场与远场传播区域，并且识别结果与理论传播边界相吻合，说明其具有较好的稳健性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 论文证明了基于子阵列空间接收功率变化和OPTICS无监督聚类，可以在不依赖信道状态信息及预先通信距离的情况下识别UM-MIMO中的近场和远场区域。该方法为传播区域自适应的波束成形、信道估计及后续距离估计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Signal processing techniques for wireless communications and sensing fundamentally differ between near-field and far-field propagation regimes. Accurately identifying the applicable propagation region is therefore essential for enabling efficient beamforming and channel estimation in ultra-massive MIMO (UM-MIMO) systems. This paper proposes a fully unsupervised learning framework to distinguish near-field from far-field propagation based solely on received signal measurements, before estimating the communication distance, and without relying on channel state information. The proposed approach exploits spatial signal power variations across subarrays of a UM array as a physics-inspired feature extraction stage, followed by the OPTICS clustering algorithm to infer the communication region. Simulation results under various system configurations and signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) levels demonstrate that the proposed method accurately identifies the near-field and far-field regions, showing agreement with theoretical boundaries.

</details>


### [143] [Exploiting Mutual Coupling Structure for Channel Estimation of Active RIS-Assisted Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21062)
*Simon Tarboush,Pinjun Zheng,Hadi Sarieddeen,Tareq Y. Al-Naffouri,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究具有密集集成单元的有源可重构智能表面（RIS）辅助通信链路中的互耦（MC）感知信道估计问题。作者基于散射参数建立物理一致的MC模型，并将信道估计表述为压缩感知问题；针对MC导致感知矩阵维度和计算复杂度显著增加的问题，利用散射矩阵结构与互耦机制设计低复杂度、缩减规模的感知矩阵。数值结果表明，该方法相比忽略互耦的方法性能提升数个dB，同时接近完整互耦感知估计器的精度，但复杂度显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集集成RIS单元之间存在显著的互耦效应。若信道建模和估计忽略互耦，将导致估计精度下降，难以充分发挥有源RIS的性能。另一方面，将互耦纳入压缩感知模型会扩大感知矩阵规模，显著增加计算复杂度。因此，需要一种既能准确处理互耦效应、又能降低信道估计复杂度的方法。

Method: 首先，采用基于散射参数的物理一致模型描述有源RIS辅助链路中的互耦效应。随后，将考虑互耦的信道估计建模为压缩感知问题。针对互耦引起的感知矩阵维度膨胀，利用散射矩阵的结构特性以及互耦机制，构造缩减规模的设计感知矩阵，并提出低复杂度信道估计器。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的方法相较于忽略互耦的估计器可获得数个dB的性能增益；在估计精度方面接近完整互耦感知方案，同时显著降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 在有源RIS密集集成场景中，互耦效应必须被纳入信道建模与估计。通过利用散射矩阵和互耦结构进行降维，所提出的估计器在精度与复杂度之间实现了良好折中，能够以较低计算代价获得接近完整互耦模型的估计性能。

Abstract: Accurate channel modeling and estimation of active reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted links with densely integrated elements are essential to fully unleashing this technology's potential. This work adopts a physically consistent model incorporating mutual coupling (MC) effects, modeled via scattering parameters, in RIS-aided communication. We formulate the MC-aware channel estimation as a compressed sensing (CS) problem. The MC effect leads to an increase in the sensing matrix dimensions. This increased dimensionality substantially elevates the complexity of the formulated CS problem. To overcome this, we propose a low-complexity estimator that leverages the structure of the scattering matrix and MC mechanisms to obtain a reduced-size design sensing matrix. Numerical results demonstrate that our approach outperforms MC-unaware estimators by several dBs, achieving accuracy comparable to fully MC-aware solutions but with significantly lower complexity.

</details>


### [144] [An $m^{2.943}$ Bohnenblust--Hille Bound on the Boolean Cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21144)
*Joseph Slote,Chun-Kai Tseng,Alexander Volberg*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究布尔立方体上傅里叶次数至多为m的复值函数，证明其傅里叶系数满足q_m=2m/(m+1)范数不等式，幂损失从早期的m^9改进为接近m^{β_0}，其中β_0=3/2+1/log 2≈2.9427，并允许任意ε>0的m^{β_0+ε}上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是改进早期版本中的m^9估计，获得更接近最优的多项式增长率，并建立一套能够处理不同傅里叶层级及奇偶性约束的统一证明方案。核心目标是控制低阶与高阶傅里叶系数的累积损失，同时优化迭代或bootstrap过程中的指数。

Method: 作者采用分层傅里叶估计与bootstrap证明框架。前三个傅里叶层分别在m、m^{3/2}和m^{5/3}尺度上控制；一般层级使用包含M^{μ_r/r}、r^B及min{r,L_M}^8的权重，其中μ_r=ceil(3r/2)，L_M与log(M+1)同阶。对r<L_M采用保持奇偶性的中心窗口，对r≥L_M采用收缩的平衡窗口且不再使用奇偶性。通过精细估计高阶部分的损失—增益因子e·2^{-B}，并要求B>1/log 2，闭合整体bootstrap。

Result: 对任意ε>0，存在有限常数C_ε，使所有傅里叶次数不超过m的复值函数f:{-1,1}^n→C满足：(Σ_A |f̂(A)|^{q_m})^{1/q_m}≤C_ε m^{β_0+ε}||f||_∞，其中q_m=2m/(m+1)，β_0=3/2+1/log 2≈2.9426950408。相较早期的m^9估计，指数显著下降至约2.943。

Conclusion: 本文证明方案将傅里叶系数不等式的增长指数推进到β_0+ε，并展示了低阶分层估计、窗口选择、奇偶性处理及高阶bootstrap优化的协同作用。摘要表明该结果主要是方法和估计上的改进；关于β_0是否最优、常数及具体技术引理的完整证明，仍需结合正文进一步判断。

Abstract: Let $q_m=2m/(m+1)$ and put \[
  β_0=\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{\log 2}=2.9426950408\ldots, \] where $\log$ is the natural logarithm. We give a proof scheme showing that, for every $\varepsilon>0$, there is $C_\varepsilon<\infty$ such that every complex-valued function $f:\{-1,1\}^n\to\C$ of Fourier degree at most $m$ satisfies \[
  \left(\sum_{A\subseteq[n]}\abs{\wh f(A)}^{q_m}\right)^{1/q_m}
  \le C_\varepsilon m^{β_0+\varepsilon}\norm{f}_\infty. \] The improvement over the $m^9$ estimate of the earlier draft has two ingredients. The first three Fourier levels are estimated at the scales $m$, $m^{3/2}$, and $m^{5/3}$. These losses are encoded in the weight $M^{μ_r/r}r^B\min\{r,L_M\}^8$, $L_M\asymp_B \log (M+1)$, with $μ_r=\lceil3r/2\rceil$ for $r\ge2$. A parity-compatible central window handles $r<L_M$, while a shrinking balanced window handles $r\ge L_M\asymp_B\log(M+1)$, no parity is used in this regime. The leading high-degree loss--gain factor is $\ee\,2^{-B}$; careful uniform bounds close the bootstrap for every $B>1/\log2$. The formula for $μ_r$ is explained below.

</details>


### [145] [Performance Analysis of Cooperative Multi-Carrier Relay-Based UAV Networks Over Generalized Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21238)
*Ibrahim Y. Abualhaol,Mustafa M. Matalgah*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究广义衰落信道下协作无人机网络的中断概率与平均可达比特率。针对含地面控制单元和中继拓扑的多载波系统，利用有限混合模型与期望最大化算法近似自适应M-QAM可达比特率的概率密度函数，并据此获得中断概率和平均比特率的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作无人机网络中的不同任务对传输速率有不同最低要求，且无线链路受到广义衰落影响。系统不仅可能因任一链路速率低于阈值而中断，还可能因中继至地面控制单元的链路无法承载所有转发无人机的汇聚数据及中继自身数据而中断。因此，有必要建立能够反映多链路、异构速率需求和中继负载约束的解析性能模型。

Method: 构建包含一个地面控制单元的中继协作拓扑，允许无人机直接与地面控制单元通信或经中继通信。定义系统中断事件为：任一链路的传输速率低于对应最低阈值，或中继—地面控制单元链路无法传输所有被中继无人机的汇聚数据以及中继无人机自身所需的最低速率。首先在广义衰落信道和自适应M-QAM条件下推导协作多载波系统的可达比特率；随后采用有限混合模型与期望最大化算法近似可达比特率的概率密度函数，进一步推导中断概率和平均可达比特率的闭式或简化解析表达式。

Result: 论文得到广义衰落信道下协作多载波系统的中断概率与平均可达比特率解析表达式。有限混合—期望最大化方法为自适应M-QAM可达比特率建立了简洁的概率密度近似，从而支持中断概率和平均比特率的闭式计算。摘要未提供具体数值结果、仿真精度或与其他方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: 该研究为含中继、具有任务相关速率需求的协作UAV网络提供了可分析的性能评估框架。通过对可达比特率分布进行有限混合近似，可以降低直接处理复杂广义衰落和多链路耦合问题的难度，并有效评估系统的可靠性与吞吐性能。

Abstract: The outage probability in a network of cooperative unmanned airborne vehicles (UAVs) over generalized fading channels is studied analytically using finite mixture with expectation maximization technique. A relay-based topology with one ground control unit (GCU) is considered, where the cooperative UAVs can communicate with the GCU directly or through relay. The application the UAV assigned for specifies the minimum required transmission rate the UAV should achieve. The outage probability of the system is defined as the probability that either the transmission rate over any of the links drops below a predefined minimum threshold for that link or the Relay-GCU link is not able to transmit the aggregate data from all relayed UAVs and the minimum rate required by the relay UAV itself. Throughout the paper, expressions for the outage probability and the average achievable bit rate of a cooperative multi-carrier system are derived over generalized fading channels. Finite Mixture with Expectation-Maximization algorithm is utilized to derive a simple approximate expression for the probability density function (pdf) of the achievable bit rate assuming adaptive M-ary quadrature amplitude modulation (M-QAM). This pdf is used to derive closed-form expressions for the outage probability and the average bit rate.

</details>


### [146] [Co-Evolving Zero-Day Jamming: Adaptive Attack Synthesis and Graph Attention-Based Online Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21334)
*Ghilas Aissou,Rémi A. Chou,Taejoon Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一个双层框架：利用结合图注意力网络与DP-means的在线检测器识别已知及零日干扰策略，并利用基于推理的强化学习干扰器构建更具现实性的黑盒对抗评测基准。实验表明，RL干扰器在攻击效能和隐蔽性方面优于基线，检测框架也取得更高检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零日干扰检测评估存在两方面不足：一是攻击模型通常假设掌握目标接收机的先验信息，难以作为通用、现实的评测基准；二是现有检测器无法建模干扰行为的全局时频结构，也难以及时区分新出现的零日干扰策略。因此，论文旨在同时改进对抗性评测模型与在线检测能力。

Method: 论文包含两部分方法。其一，提出在线检测框架：使用图注意力网络（GAT）学习干扰行为的时频表示，并结合Dirichlet过程均值（DP-means）聚类，在统一学习目标下同时完成已知策略分类与零日策略发现。其二，提出基于推理的强化学习干扰器：将目标接收机视为黑盒，通过假设检验推断检测器状态，并优化攻击影响与隐蔽性之间的权衡。

Result: 仿真结果显示，相较基线方法，所提出的RL干扰器攻击效能提高33%，隐蔽性提高67%；针对该RL干扰器，所提出的检测框架检测准确率较基线提高20%。

Conclusion: 该研究通过联合设计更现实的黑盒对抗干扰器和能够发现新策略的在线检测器，缓解了零日干扰评测与检测中的关键局限。结果表明，GAT与DP-means的结合有助于捕获全局时频结构并识别新策略，而推理驱动的RL模型可提供更严格、更接近实际的检测评测环境。

Abstract: Effective evaluation of zero-day jamming detectors requires robust adversarial models. However, existing attack models often assume prior knowledge of the target receiver, limiting their utility as evaluation benchmarks. On the detection side, existing detectors fail to capture the global temporal-spectral structure of jamming behavior and cannot differentiate zero-day strategies as they emerge. This paper addresses these limitations through a two-pronged framework. First, an online detection framework is introduced that combines a graph attention network (GAT) for temporal-spectral representation learning with Dirichlet process (DP)-means clustering. This framework jointly classifies known and discovers zero-day strategies within a unified learning objective. Second, an inference-driven reinforcement learning (RL) jammer is proposed as an adversarial benchmark. The jammer treats the target receiver as a black-box, infers the detector state via hypothesis testing, and optimizes the trade-off between attack impact and stealth. Simulation results show that the proposed RL jammer outperforms benchmarks, achieving 33% higher attack efficacy and 67% higher stealth. The proposed detection framework against the proposed RL jammer is shown to achieve 20% higher detection accuracy than the benchmarks.

</details>


### [147] [Explicit Constructions of Maximum-Cardinality Families of Plateaued Functions with Pairwise Disjoint Walsh Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21389)
*Chen Wang,Shuailong Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架下的两类显式代数构造，生成最大规模、Walsh支集两两不交且无非零线性结构的(q+1)-plateaued布尔函数族，并可控制所有成员的共同代数次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有两项性质的plateaued布尔函数族：族规模达到最大、且成员不含非零线性结构。这类函数族可用于密码学布尔函数的二次构造。已有一般性最优构造主要是谱方法，通常不给出显式代数正规形，也缺少统一设定共同代数次数的方法，因此需要显式且可调次数的代数构造。

Method: 设p≥2、q≥0且q<2^p-p-1，令m=p+q。论文在统一框架下提出两种广义Maiorana–McFarland显式代数构造：第一种基于线性函数，生成定义在m+p+1个变量上的函数；第二种基于带有bent分量的部分线性函数，生成定义在n+p+1个变量上的函数，其中n>m且n-m为偶数。两种构造均通过参数约束和组合项选择来实现预先指定的共同代数次数。

Result: 两种构造都得到规模为2^{q+1}的最大规模(q+1)-plateaued布尔函数族，成员的Walsh支集两两不交，且无成员具有非零线性结构。第一种构造可实现3≤d≤p+1，条件为q<Σ_{i=2}^{d-1} C(p,i)，最大次数p+1且达到最优。第二种可实现3≤d≤p+(n-m)/2，条件为q<Σ_{i=2}^{min{d-1,p}} C(p,i)，最大次数p+(n-m)/2，达到次优。

Conclusion: 本文弥补了既有谱构造在显式代数表达和共同代数次数控制方面的不足，提供了两类统一、显式且参数可调的构造。结果表明，第一类在代数次数上达到理论最优，第二类在更高维变量设置下获得接近最优的次数，同时保留最大族规模、Walsh支集不交和无非零线性结构等密码学性质。

Abstract: Families of plateaued Boolean functions with pairwise disjoint Walsh supports are useful in secondary constructions of cryptographic Boolean functions. Of particular interest are maximum-cardinality families whose members admit no nonzero linear structures. To the best of our knowledge, the previously known general construction attaining both properties is spectral (Hodžić et al., IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 65(9): 5865--5879, 2019). In that work, explicit algebraic normal forms are not generally provided, and no general method is established for prescribing a common algebraic degree for all family members.
  In this paper, we present two new explicit algebraic constructions within a unified framework, one based on linear functions and the other on partially linear functions with bent components. Let $p\geq 2$ and $q\geq 0$ satisfy $q<2^p-p-1$, and set $m=p+q$. Both constructions yield maximum-cardinality families of $2^{q+1}$ $(q+1)$-plateaued Boolean functions with pairwise disjoint Walsh supports. No member admits a nonzero linear structure, and every member has an explicit generalized Maiorana--McFarland representation.
  The first construction produces functions in $m+p+1$ variables and realizes any prescribed common algebraic degree $3\leq d\leq p+1$, provided that $q<\sum_{i=2}^{d-1}\binom{p}{i}$; its maximum attainable degree $p+1$ is optimal. The second construction produces functions in $n+p+1$ variables, where $n>m$ and $n-m$ is even, and realizes any prescribed common algebraic degree $3\leq d\leq p+(n-m)/2$, provided that $q<\sum_{i=2}^{\min\{d-1,p\}}\binom{p}{i}$; its maximum attainable degree $p+(n-m)/2$ is next-to-optimal.

</details>


### [148] [Constant-List Insertion--Deletion Codes:New Bounds and an Improvement of Levenshtein's Lower Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21395)
*Han Mao Kiah,Hengjia Wei,Ruixiao Zeng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究固定列表大小 L 下可纠正对抗性插入与删除的编码，提出插入—删除误差之间的组合归约，改进二进制码的可达率下界，并给出任意固定字母表上的上界。其核心成果包括更强的二进制唯一译码率下界、固定列表大小下的插入/删除下界，以及改进的高阶 Elias 型上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插入删除码的结果在固定列表大小、混合误差以及一般字母表情形下仍存在明显界限差距，且经典 Levenshtein 率下界并不理想。本文旨在建立统一的组合分析框架，显式刻画列表大小 L 的影响，并改进二进制码的可达率与一般字母表的上界。

Method: 首先建立组合归约：在解码保证不变且不增加列表大小的前提下，用 L 个插入预算单位换取 1 个删除预算单位，从而将混合误差问题归约到插入-only 下界和删除-only 上界。下界证明结合 Lovász 局部引理与按 run 数采样的码字构造，并利用生成函数估计 L+1 个采样词共享某个允许接收词的概率。上界方面，一方面采用 run counting 得到 Levenshtein 型界；另一方面通过分析将 L+1 个码字嵌入公共超序列的位置集合的交并，构造高阶 Elias 型上界。

Result: 对二进制唯一译码，本文严格改进 Levenshtein 在所有满足经典表达式非负的 0<δ<1/2 区间内的率下界；当 δ=0.1 时，下界由约 0.162009 提升至 0.180431，相对提升约 11.37%。同时得到任意固定列表大小的插入和删除率下界，并给出任意固定字母表大小 q≥2 的上界。高阶 Elias 型上界在 L=1 时恢复 Yasunaga 的渐近唯一译码上界，并对每个固定 L 和 0<γ<q−1 严格改进 Haeupler–Shahrasbi–Sudan 的插入上界。

Conclusion: 本文通过插入—删除预算归约和基于 run 的概率构造，统一推进了固定列表大小下的同步编辑码理论。结果显著改善了二进制唯一译码的已知率下界，并强化了一般列表译码与任意固定字母表上的上界；数值比较表明理论界限有所收紧，但可达率与上界之间仍存在进一步缩小的空间。

Abstract: We study codes correcting adversarial insertions and deletions with list size $L$ fixed independently of the block length. We derive new achievable-rate bounds for binary codes and upper bounds over every fixed alphabet of size $q\ge2$, retaining explicit dependence on $L$.
  We establish a combinatorial reduction that trades $L$ units of insertion budget for one unit of deletion budget in the decoding guarantee, without changing the code or increasing the list size. Consequently, asymptotic bounds for mixed errors with insertion fraction $γ$ and deletion fraction $δ$ follow from insertion-only lower bounds at $γ+Lδ$ and deletion-only upper bounds at $δ+γ/L$.
  For binary unique decoding, we strictly improve Levenshtein's classical asymptotic rate lower bound for every deletion fraction $0<δ<1/2$ for which the classical rate expression is nonnegative. At $δ=0.1$, the lower bound increases from approximately $0.162009$ to $0.180431$, a relative increase of about $11.37\%$. Our framework also yields insertion and deletion lower bounds for every fixed list size. The existence proofs combine the Lovász local lemma with sampling from words having a specified number of runs, where a run is a maximal block of equal symbols. Generating functions provide refined bounds on the probability that $L+1$ sampled words share an allowed received word.
  We also derive a Levenshtein-type upper bound by run counting and, separately, a higher-order Elias bound using intersections and unions of the position sets used to embed $L+1$ codewords in a common supersequence. The latter recovers Yasunaga's asymptotic unique-decoding bound at $L=1$ and strictly improves the Haeupler--Shahrasbi--Sudan insertion bound for every fixed $L$ and $0<γ<q-1$. Numerical comparisons quantify the gains and remaining gaps.

</details>


### [149] [Interference-Driven Clustered Optimisation for FM Spectrum Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21441)
*Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大规模跨境FM频谱协调的干扰驱动聚类优化框架，通过识别主导干扰发射机、构建干扰图并划分发射机簇，将高维全局功率控制问题分解为多个较小的优化任务，再结合全局细化，以较低计算复杂度实现外部广播业务保护与本地覆盖维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨境FM频谱协调需要同时保护外国广播业务和维持本国覆盖。随着规划数据规模扩大，包含数千个发射机及数百万个发射机—像素关系，传统功率控制优化面临高维度、高计算量和较长运行时间的问题。因此，需要一种能够利用干扰结构、降低优化规模并适用于实际规划场景的方法。

Method: 首先分析受保护业务，识别对保护违规起主要作用的主导干扰发射机，并量化其干扰影响；随后将发射机之间的干扰关系表示为干扰图，从中提取面向优化的发射机聚类。各聚类分别采用聚类模拟退火求解局部功率控制任务，之后通过全局细化处理聚类之间的剩余相互作用。覆盖和干扰评估采用与频率相关的保护准则及动态最强业务分配模型；在工程实现上使用稀疏矩阵和GPU加速，以支持大规模计算。

Result: 在真实跨境FM协调场景中的测试表明，所提出的聚类策略能够显著降低优化复杂度和运行时间，同时保持对外国广播业务的保护以及本国覆盖水平。此外，该方法能够生成对有害干扰贡献最大的发射机排序，为优化过程和频谱规划提供可解释依据。

Conclusion: 该框架有效利用了跨境FM干扰关系的稀疏性和局部主导特征，将难以直接求解的大规模全局功率控制问题转化为可管理的聚类优化问题。结合全局细化、稀疏计算和GPU加速后，该方法兼顾了计算效率、协调性能与结果可解释性，具有面向实际大规模频谱规划部署的潜力。

Abstract: Cross-border FM spectrum coordination involves protecting foreign broadcasting services while preserving domestic coverage, amid increasingly large radio-planning datasets containing thousands of transmitters and millions of transmitter-pixel relationships. In such scenarios, conventional optimisation approaches become computationally demanding due to the high dimensionality of the associated power-control problem. This paper proposes an interference-driven clustered optimisation framework for large-scale FM spectrum coordination. The proposed method exploits the observation that violations of foreign-service protection are typically dominated by a limited subset of transmitters. Protected services are therefore analysed to identify dominant interferers and quantify their impact on interference. These relationships are represented through an interference graph from which optimisation-oriented transmitter clusters are extracted. The clusters decompose the global power-control problem into smaller optimisation tasks solved with clustered simulated annealing, followed by a global refinement that captures residual inter-cluster interactions. Coverage and interference are evaluated using frequency-dependent protection criteria and a dynamic strongest-service assignment model. To enable operational-scale planning, the framework uses sparse matrices and GPU-accelerated computations. Tests on realistic cross-border FM coordination scenarios show that the clustering strategy greatly reduces optimisation complexity and runtime while maintaining foreign-service protection and domestic coverage. The method also yields an interpretable ranking of transmitters that contribute most to harmful interference, supporting optimisation and spectrum planning.

</details>


### [150] [Sharp High-Entropy Bounds for Sums of Independent Discrete Random Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21459)
*Haoran Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明了独立离散随机变量之和的高熵下界：在无挠阿贝尔群以及适当渐近条件下的素数阶循环群中，H(X+Y)至少为(H(X)+H(Y))/2+1/2-o(1)，并证明常数1/2最优，同时给出显式误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于和的熵下界结果主要针对同分布求和；对于任意独立求和，已有普适加性常数仅为1/8。作者旨在证明此前猜想的尖锐常数1/2，并将结果推广到无挠阿贝尔群和素数阶循环群。

Method: 首先从离散分布中提取一个具有成对点概率结构的分量，并控制剩余部分的熵，使该控制不依赖剩余分量的支撑大小。随后利用离散重排和均匀扰动，将连续情形的熵幂不等式转移到所提取的分量上。在素数阶循环群中，进一步估计模约化造成的熵损失，从而得到相应结论及显式误差界。

Result: 对有限香农熵的独立离散随机变量X,Y，在任意无挠阿贝尔群中，当max{H(X),H(Y)}趋于无穷时，有H(X+Y)≥(H(X)+H(Y))/2+1/2-o(1)。在素数阶循环群F_p中，只要max{H(X),H(Y)}以及log_2 p−max{H(X),H(Y)}都趋于无穷，同样的结论成立。论文还给出了两类群中的显式误差界。

Conclusion: 独立求和的渐近高熵下界中的加性常数确实为1/2，而非此前已知的1/8；二项分布例子表明该常数不可改进，因此结果是尖锐的。

Abstract: Sharp high-entropy lower bounds for the entropy of a sum were known for identically distributed summands in torsion-free abelian groups and in prime cyclic groups. For arbitrary independent summands, Gavalakis, Goh and Kontoyiannis obtained an additive constant of $1/8$ and conjectured that the sharp constant is $1/2$. We prove that independent discrete random variables $X,Y$ with finite Shannon entropies satisfy $H(X+Y)\ge (H(X)+H(Y))/2+1/2-o(1)$ in every torsion-free abelian group as $\max{H(X),H(Y)}\to\infty$. The same conclusion holds in the prime cyclic group $\mathbb F_p$ when both $\max{H(X),H(Y)}$ and $\log_2 p-\max{H(X),H(Y)}$ tend to infinity. We give explicit error bounds in both settings.
  The proof extracts a component with paired point probabilities while controlling the entropy of the remainder independently of its support. Discrete rearrangement and uniform perturbation then transfer the continuous entropy power inequality to this component. In prime cyclic groups, an additional estimate controls the entropy lost under modular reduction. Binomial distributions show that the constant $1/2$ is optimal.

</details>


### [151] [Orbital Detection: On Maximum-Entropy Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21466)
*Kuranage Roche Rayan Ranasinghe,Takumi Takahashi,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最大熵轨道先验的软输入检测方法，将离散星座上的逐符号检测复杂度从 O(M) 降至 O(L)，其中 L 是不同幅度环的数量，同时保持接近最优检测的符号错误率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软输入 MMSE 和 MAP 检测需要遍历星座中的 M 个符号，计算复杂度较高，尤其在高阶调制下更为明显。作者希望在尽可能保留星座幅度统计信息的前提下，对相位采用最小承诺的先验表示，从而降低检测复杂度。

Method: 将离散星座先验替换为满足相同幅度边缘分布的最大熵先验。该先验由多个均匀圆环的混合构成，并通过混合离散—连续熵最大化得到。作者证明这是在精确保留幅度统计的条件下、相位最无偏的唯一复平面分布。在 AWGN 信道下，推导出后验分布的闭式表达：幅度环之间形成 softmax，给定观测后的相位服从 von Mises 分布。由此构造轨道 MMSE 与 MAP 检测器，并采用分层规则：先按后验质量选择环，再按条件众数选择相位。同时分析成对环边界与联合后验密度边界的差异，并推导高 SNR 下边界向外偏移的首阶近似。

Result: 检测复杂度由每个符号 O(M) 降至 O(L)，其中 L≤M。轨道先验能够产生闭式后验、MMSE 和 MAP 检测表达式。数值实验表明，在标准星座上，轨道检测器的 SER 与最优检测器相近，但计算复杂度显著更低。

Conclusion: 最大熵轨道先验在保留离散星座幅度统计的同时，以连续均匀相位近似离散相位，从而实现低复杂度软输入检测。该方法在性能与复杂度之间取得了较好的折中，尤其适用于星座规模较大但不同幅度环数量较少的场景；其潜在局限是相位离散结构被忽略，因而在某些低 SNR、特殊星座或对精确相位判决敏感的场景下可能存在性能损失。

Abstract: Soft-input detection over a discrete constellation \(\mathcal{M}\) of cardinality \(M\) requires computing a posterior whose mean and mode are respectively given by the minimum mean square error (MMSE) and maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimates, both of which incur a computational cost of order \(\mathcal{O}(M)\) per symbol. We show that this cost is reduced to \(\mathcal{O}(L)\), where \(L \le M\) is the number of distinct amplitudes (rings), once the discrete prior is replaced by its maximum-entropy counterpart subject to the same radial marginal. This orbital prior, which is a mixture of uniform circular shells, is obtained by maximizing a mixed discrete-continuous entropy. We prove in this paper that such a distribution is the only distribution on \(\mathbb{C}\) that preserves the amplitude statistics of \(\mathcal{M}\) exactly while remaining maximally noncommittal in phase. Under the additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel, the orbital prior induces a closed-form posterior that factors into a softmax over the \(L\) rings and a von Mises phase distribution whose concentration is supplied entirely by the observation, yielding closed-form orbital MMSE and MAP detectors of the discrete symbol at \(\mathcal{O}(L)\) cost. The resulting hierarchical rule selects the ring by posterior mass and the phase by conditional mode. We compare the pairwise ring boundary with that of the joint posterior-density and quantify the leading-order outward shift at high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Numerical results using standard constellations confirm that the orbital detectors maintain similar symbol error rate (SER) performance to optimal detectors, at a fraction of the complexity.

</details>


### [152] [On the Fourier Entropy-Influence Conjecture for Boolean Plateaued Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21563)
*Vladimir N. Potapov*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了布尔函数的一个新的权重—代数次数不等式，并以此证明傅里叶熵—影响力（FEI）猜想在 plateaued 函数和 partially bent 函数类上的成立，分别得到尖锐常数 4 和 2；同时给出若干 p-偏置布尔超立方体上的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FEI 猜想旨在建立布尔函数傅里叶谱的熵与总影响力之间的普适关系。对于 plateaued 函数和 partially bent 函数，如何利用其代数结构获得最优或尖锐的 FEI 常数仍需解决；此外，相关结论在 p-偏置分布下的推广也具有理论价值。

Method: 首先证明不等式 2∑_{x∈F_2^n} f(x)wt(x) ≥ wt(f)(n−deg(f))，将函数取值的汉明重量、输入点重量与代数次数联系起来。随后利用该不等式及 plateaued、partially bent 函数的傅里叶谱和代数结构，分别推导 FEI 猜想及其尖锐常数；最后将分析推广到布尔超立方体上的 p-偏置分布，得到若干估计。

Result: FEI 猜想对 plateaued 函数成立，且该函数类的尖锐 FEI 常数为 4；对 partially bent 函数也成立，且相应尖锐常数为 2。此外，论文给出了若干关于 p-偏置分布的估计。核心不等式为 2∑_{x∈F_2^n}f(x)wt(x)≥wt(f)(n−deg(f))。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一个可连接汉明重量与代数次数的基础不等式，并据此解决了两类重要布尔函数上的 FEI 猜想。结果不仅给出 plateaued 函数和 partially bent 函数的最优常数，还为 p-偏置情形下的进一步研究提供了估计工具。

Abstract: We prove the following inequality for Boolean functions: $2\sum_{x\in F_2^n}f(x)wt(x)\geq wt(f)(n-deg(f))$. Using this inequality, we establish the Fourier Entropy-Influence (FEI) conjecture for Boolean plateaued functions. In particular, we show that the sharp FEI constant for the class of plateaued functions is 4. We also prove the FEI conjecture for partially bent functions and show that the corresponding sharp constant is 2. Finally, we derive several estimates for the p-biased distribution on the Boolean hypercube.
  Keywords: Fourier entropy, total influence, average sensitivity, plateaued function, algebraic degree, Reed-Muller code, p-biased distribution.

</details>


### [153] [Goal-Oriented Communication and Control Co-Design via Semantic Push-Pull in Industrial IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21566)
*Muhammad Azeem Khan,Yuriy Zacchia Lun,Aamir Mahmood,Piergiuseppe Di Marco,Mikael Gidlund,Fortunato Santucci*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向6G工业物联网无线网络化控制系统的通信—控制协同调度框架：利用语义层分别协调上行传感器推送与下行控制器拉取，以状态到误差比（SER）衡量数据的实际控制价值，并结合信噪比（SNR）评估链路可靠性，同时依据各控制对象的局部动态缩放执行死区。在瑞利衰落信道仿真中，该方法以低于周期调度的通信开销获得接近周期调度的跟踪精度，并缓解纯事件触发方法的估计误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G工业物联网中的无线资源有限，而不同控制回路需要持续保持稳定和准确。周期调度或基于信息新鲜度（AoI）的策略虽然能控制数据陈旧度，却会造成持续的信道占用；纯事件触发策略能够减少传输，但传感器侧阈值可能无法及时反映关键状态变化，导致控制性能恶化甚至失稳。因此，需要一种同时考虑控制影响、无线链路可靠性和异构被控对象动态特性的通信—控制协同机制。

Method: 构建由6G语义层驱动的通信—控制协同框架，分别仲裁上行和下行资源。用状态到误差比（SER）衡量数据包对控制性能的实际影响，而非仅依据信息新鲜度；将SER所体现的控制置信度与信噪比（SNR）所体现的链路可靠性结合，设计基于阈值的传感器推送机制和状态感知的控制器拉取机制。针对动态特性不同的控制对象，根据本地装置动力学显式缩放执行死区，以实现更公平的资源分配。通过瑞利衰落信道下的仿真进行评估。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法能够改变传输速率与控制质量之间的帕累托前沿：在显著降低通信开销的同时，获得与周期调度相近的跟踪精度；相较于纯事件触发策略，还能减轻由状态信息不足或估计偏差导致的控制误差。摘要未提供具体的数值增益、系统规模、基线参数或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，仅追求数据新鲜度或最小化传输次数都不是无线网络化控制的充分目标；将数据的控制语义价值、链路可靠性和被控对象动力学联合纳入调度，更有望实现通信效率与控制性能的平衡。该框架适用于资源受限且控制对象异构的6G工业物联网场景，但其有效性仍需通过理论稳定性证明、更多信道与负载条件、实验平台验证以及对阈值和模型失配鲁棒性的分析进一步确认。

Abstract: Emerging 6G industrial IoT architectures require wireless networked control systems capable of stabilizing diverse control loops over tightly constrained radio resources. Conventional periodic and Age-of-Information (AoI) based scheduling guarantees bounded staleness at the cost of persistent channel saturation. Conversely, pure event-triggered (PureET) strategies minimize transmissions but risk catastrophic silent deterioration when local sensor-side thresholds fail to reflect critical state evolution. To bridge this gap, we propose a communication-control co-design framework governed by a 6G Semantic Layer that independently arbitrates uplink and downlink resources. Instead of relying on freshness, our architecture evaluates the actual control impact of a packet using the state-to-error ratio (SER). We unify this control confidence with channel reliability in terms of signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) to orchestrate a threshold-based sensor push and a state-aware controller pull mechanism. To ensure equitable resource allocation across dynamically heterogeneous plants, the proposed framework explicitly scales actuation deadbands according to local plant dynamics. Simulations over Rayleigh-faded channels demonstrate that this approach fundamentally shifts the Pareto frontier between transmission rate and control quality. The proposed scheme achieves tracking accuracy comparable to periodic schedulers at a reduced communication overhead, while mitigating the estimation errors characteristic of PureET.

</details>


### [154] [New lower bounds for kissing numbers in dimensions $25$--$29$ and $31$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21591)
*Rustem Takhanov,Stanislav Yun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文改进了25—31维球接吻数的下界构造，分别得到τ25≥197058、τ26≥198552、τ27≥200046、τ28≥204522、τ29≥209497和τ31≥238354。核心是在Leech lifting构造中利用某些部分之间没有接触的几何自由度，通过旋转、非正交对角线性变形及局部坐标旋转，加入新的接吻点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前25—31维的最佳构造主要由24维最优接吻构型的Leech lifting得到。论文旨在利用该提升构型中“未提升主体”与“提升及辅助向量块”之间不存在接触这一结构性空隙，进一步放松几何约束，从而在多个维度加入更多与中心球相切且彼此不重叠的球，提升已知接吻数下界。

Method: 以Leech lifting构型为基础，将构型划分为未提升的bulk与由提升向量、辅助向量组成的block。首先固定bulk并旋转block，在26、27、28维加入两个对跖点，在29维加入一个点。随后进行两类改进：在25维对提升block施加非正交的对角线性变形，以容纳两个对跖点；在31维仅旋转提升向量的附加坐标，以容纳四个非对跖点。通过验证新增点与原有点之间的距离约束，确保构型仍为有效接吻配置。

Result: 得到新的下界：τ25≥197058，τ26≥198552，τ27≥200046，τ28≥204522，τ29≥209497，τ31≥238354。摘要明确说明26—29维的增益来自block旋转，25维来自非正交对角变形，31维来自附加坐标的局部旋转。

Conclusion: 论文表明，Leech lifting构型中的非接触关系不仅是构造的副产物，也可作为系统优化接吻配置的几何自由度。针对不同维度采用定制的变换，可以突破此前下界，说明局部旋转与线性变形是改进高维球接吻构造的有效工具。

Abstract: The kissing number in dimension $d$ is the largest number of non-overlapping congruent spheres that can simultaneously touch a central sphere of the same size. We study dimensions $25$-$31$, where the best previous constructions are based on Leech lifting from the optimal kissing configuration in dimension $24$. Our method exploits the absence of contacts between the unlifted bulk and the block consisting of lifted and auxiliary vectors. Rotating this block while keeping the bulk fixed creates room for two antipodal points in dimensions $26$, $27$, and $28$, and one point in dimension $29$.
  Two further modifications yield improvements in dimensions $25$ and $31$: a nonorthogonal diagonal linear deformation of the lifted block admits two antipodal points in dimension $25$, while rotating only the additional coordinates of the lifted vectors admits four nonantipodal points in dimension $31$. Together, these constructions yield the new lower bounds $τ_{25}\geq 197058$, $τ_{26}\geq 198552$, $τ_{27}\geq 200046$, $τ_{28}\geq 204522$, $τ_{29}\geq 209497$, and $τ_{31}\geq 238354$.

</details>


### [155] [Resolution of an Open Problem on Quasi-Cyclic Codes over $\mathbb{Z}_4$ and New Quaternary Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21601)
*Nuh Aydin,Aditya Tyagi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究奇数长度下，如何判断由四元数环Z_4上的循环码构造的准循环码是否继承原循环码的类型，并给出充要判据及其编码应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z_4不是域，因此由循环码C_g通过生成元(f_1g,...,f_ell g)构造的准循环码C，未必保持C_g的类型4^{k_1}2^{k_2}。此前关于何时保持类型的问题尚未解决，本文旨在给出系统的充分条件、必要充分条件以及可操作的判定方法。

Method: 首先给出两类保持类型的充分条件：其一是某个f_i在F_2上与x^m-1互素，其二是所有f_i整体与x^m-1互素。随后利用m为奇数时x^m-1无重因子的性质，通过中国剩余定理将Z_4[x]/<x^m-1>分解为有限链环的直积。在该分解下，分析各不可约因子对应分量上f_i的作用，得到仅依赖于g未消失因子的类型保持充要条件；进一步据此导出C成为自由Z_4模的判定，并用Magma进行计算搜索。

Result: 理论上，论文解决了此前提出的开放问题：两种充分条件被证明有效，但一般并非必要；同时获得了一个关于f_1,...,f_ell的必要且充分条件，可精确判断C是否与C_g具有相同类型。应用方面，基于该判据搜索得到大量新的Z_4上的准循环码，其中一些码的Lee距离超过了同类型的已有最佳记录。

Conclusion: 本文建立了奇数长度Z_4准循环码类型继承的完整判定框架，将问题转化为x^m-1不可约因子分量上的局部条件。该结果不仅解决了理论开放问题，还提供了自由性测试和指导计算机搜索高Lee距离QC码的实用工具。

Abstract: Given a cyclic code $C_g = \langle g(x) \rangle$ of odd length $m$ over $\mathbb{Z}_4$, one common way to build a quasi-cyclic (QC) code is to pick $f_1, \dots, f_\ell \in \mathbb{Z}_4[x]$ and let $C = \langle (f_1 g, \dots, f_\ell g) \rangle$. Because $\mathbb{Z}_4$ is not a field, the type of $C_g$ ($4^{k_1}2^{k_2}$) is not necessarily inherited by $C$. Determining conditions under which the type of $C_g$ is inherited by $C$ was posed as an open problem recently in \cite{AydinLuOnta2023}. In this paper, we settle this problem. We first give two sufficient conditions for the type to be preserved: one requires a single $f_i$ to be coprime to $x^m-1$ over $\mathbb{F}_2$, the other only requires the $f_i$ to be jointly coprime to it. Neither condition is necessary in general. Using the fact that $x^m-1$ is squarefree for odd $m$, we decompose $\mathbb{Z}_4[x]/\langle x^m-1\rangle$ into a product of finite chain rings using Chinese remainder theorem (CRT) and derive a condition on $f_1, \dots, f_\ell$ that is both necessary and sufficient for $C$ to match the type of $C_g$. This condition depends only on the irreducible factors of $x^m-1$ where $g$ does not already vanish. This also yields a simple test for when $C$ is a free $\mathbb{Z}_4$-module. Finally, we report many new QC codes over $\mathbb{Z}_4$, found by computer searches using Magma software~\cite{Magma1997} guided by this criterion, with Lee distances greater than previously known codes of the same type.

</details>


### [156] [Connections Between Quadratic Transform for Fractional Programming and Schur Complement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21730)
*Kaiming Shen,Kareem M. Attiah,Yannan Chen,Wei Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文揭示了分式规划中的二次变换与矩阵分析中的Schur补之间的深层联系，并将其推广到广义矩阵逆情形，应用于高斯向量广播信道和上行–下行对偶容量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分式规划中的二次变换与Schur补通常被视为不同工具，但二者之间的理论联系尚未被系统阐明。尤其在最不利噪声协方差矩阵奇异时，基于普通矩阵逆的KKT分析会受到输入空间和输出空间处理的限制，因此需要一种能够处理广义矩阵逆并保持信息论对偶结构的统一框架。

Method: 论文从两个角度建立联系：一是将二次变换与Schur补保证矩阵正半定性的线性矩阵不等式条件对应起来；二是将二次变换与Schur补的矩阵行列式公式联系起来。作者证明二次变换与Schur补LMI条件可以相互推出，并据此重新解释辅助变量、重新推导行列式公式，同时将两种方法推广到广义矩阵逆。最后，将广义分式规划应用于高斯向量广播信道和最不利噪声的极小极大和容量问题。

Result: 理论上，论文建立了二次变换与Schur补LMI条件之间的等价联系，并由此得到对二次变换辅助变量的新解释和Schur补行列式公式的新推导。方法还被推广到包含广义矩阵逆的情形。在应用层面，即使最不利噪声协方差矩阵奇异，广义分式规划仍能直接给出奇异矩阵分式的辅助变量表示，进而导出互易多址信道并恢复广播信道和容量的上行–下行对偶关系。

Conclusion: 本文构建了分式规划与矩阵分析之间的统一理论桥梁，表明二次变换不仅是优化技巧，也可视为Schur补结构的另一种表达。该统一视角支持广义矩阵逆和奇异矩阵场景，并为高斯向量广播信道容量问题提供了更稳健、结构清晰的分析工具。

Abstract: This paper shows that there are intimate connections between the quadratic transform technique for solving fractional programming (FP) problems and the Schur-complement technique in matrix analysis. We demonstrate that the quadratic transform technique is related to two aspects of the Schur complement: (i) the linear matrix inequality (LMI) condition for positive semidefiniteness and (ii) the matrix determinant formula. Specifically, we establish that the quadratic transform and the Schur-complement LMI condition imply each other. This connection allows us to provide new interpretations of the auxiliary variable in the quadratic transform, and it allows us to rederive the Schur-complement determinant formula. Furthermore, this connection leads to generalizations of the quadratic transform in FP and the Schur-complement LMI that can accommodate generalized matrix inverse. As an application in information theory, we apply the generalized FP framework to the least-favorable-noise minimax formulation of the Gaussian vector broadcast channel sum capacity problem. When the least-favorable noise covariance is singular, matrix-inverse-based Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) analysis would require a careful analysis of the input and output spaces of the channel. We show using generalized FP that an auxiliary-variable representation of the singular matrix fraction directly yields the reciprocal multiple-access channel and recovers the uplink-downlink duality relation for sum capacity.

</details>


### [157] [Minimum distances of primitive narrow-sense BCH codes via good zero-sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21994)
*Run Zheng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究原始窄义BCH码最小距离的精确确定问题，提出并利用F_q-good零点集，证明其与码达到设计距离之间的等价关系，并据此构造多类达到设计距离的BCH码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始窄义BCH码的最小距离通常只有设计距离下界，精确值长期难以确定。论文旨在建立可验证、可构造的判据，以判断一大类BCH码何时精确达到设计距离，并扩展已知结果的覆盖范围。

Method: 引入F_q-good零点集，并通过其消去多项式满足的导数条件刻画该性质。证明：长度为q^m-1、设计距离为δ的q元原始窄义BCH码的最小距离等于δ，当且仅当有限域F_{q^m}中存在基数为δ+1的F_q-good零点集。随后采用多项式代换、幂映射、平移逆映射及特殊形式多项式等方法，从初始good零点集构造出更多不同基数的good零点集。

Result: 建立了BCH码最小距离等于设计距离的充要条件，并构造出覆盖较广设计距离范围的多类原始窄义BCH码族。所得结果包含若干已知最小距离结论作为特例，同时给出新的good零点集及相应的新码族。

Conclusion: F_q-good零点集为研究原始窄义BCH码精确最小距离提供了统一且有效的框架。通过系统构造这类零点集，可以证明许多BCH码达到设计距离，并推进该领域中长期存在的精确最小距离问题。

Abstract: Determining the exact minimum distances of BCH codes remains a open problem. We establish the minimum distances of several families of primitive narrow-sense BCH codes, showing that they attain their designed distances. Our approach centers on $\mathbb{F}_q$-good zero-sets, which we introduce through a derivative condition on their vanishing polynomials. We show that a $q$-ary primitive narrow-sense BCH code of length $q^m-1$ and designed distance $2\leqδ\leq q^m-1$ has minimum distance $δ$ if and only if there exists an $\mathbb{F}_q$-good zero-set of cardinality $δ+1$ in the finite field $\mathbb{F}_{q^m}$ with $q^m$ elements. To construct $\mathbb{F}_q$-good zero-sets, we develop several methods based on polynomial substitutions, power maps, and shifted inverses, as well as direct constructions using polynomials of special forms. Together with suitable initial $\mathbb{F}_q$-good zero-sets, including those arising from known minimum-distance results, these methods yield new good zero-sets of various cardinalities and hence families of primitive narrow-sense BCH codes whose minimum distances equal their designed distances. These families cover a broad range of designed distances, with several known minimum-distance results recovered as special cases.

</details>


### [158] [Hermite-Fisher bounds and stability for min-entropy power inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22065)
*Silouanos Brazitikos,Martin Rapaport,Tomasz Tkocz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过变分原理与正交化Hermite多项式测试函数相结合，推导相对Fisher信息的显式下界；所得累积量界具有渐近尖锐性，并可用于高斯熵亏损及最小熵幂不等式的定量稳定性分析，同时证明了Brzezinski关于欧氏球乘积块截面的尖锐界的稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于相对Fisher信息、Gaussian熵亏损和最小熵幂不等式的尖锐不等式，但需要更显式、渐近尖锐且具有定量稳定性的下界。此外，Brzezinski关于欧氏球乘积块截面的尖锐界缺乏相应的稳定性刻画。

Method: 利用相对Fisher信息的变分原理，构造并正交化Hermite多项式测试函数，从而获得显式的累积量下界。进一步将这些下界应用于高斯熵亏损和最小熵幂不等式；同时发展用于分析欧氏球乘积块截面的稳定性方法，以证明Brzezinski尖锐界的定量稳定版本。

Result: 得到相对Fisher信息的显式下界，并证明相应累积量界具有渐近尖锐性；由此获得Gaussian熵亏损的下界，以及适用于所有维度的尖锐最小熵幂不等式的定量版本。此外，建立了Brzezinski关于欧氏球乘积块截面尖锐界的稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作统一展示了正交化Hermite测试函数在信息论不等式定量化和稳定性分析中的作用，为相对Fisher信息、Gaussian熵亏损及最小熵幂不等式提供了渐近最优的显式估计，并补充了欧氏几何不等式的稳定性理论。

Abstract: We derive explicit lower bounds for relative Fisher information by combining a variational principle with suitably orthogonalized Hermite-polynomial test functions. The resulting cumulant bounds are asymptotically sharp and yield lower bounds for Gaussian entropy deficits. We also establish quantitative versions of sharp min-entropy power inequalities in all dimensions. En route, we develop a stability result for Brzezinski's sharp bound for block sections of products of Euclidean balls, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [RBS-Attention: Radius-Bounded Sparse Prefill for Long-Context Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20971)
*Chuxu Song,Jiuqi Wei,Zhencan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 RBS-Attention，一种无需训练的双分支稀疏预填充注意力方法，通过结合块中心相关性与最大半径风险检测，缓解块中心均值稀释问题，在保持规则块稀疏 FlashAttention 执行的同时显著降低长上下文预填充成本，并保持接近稠密注意力的任务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型推理的瓶颈日益转向预填充阶段，因为稠密自注意力需要在生成前处理整个提示词。块稀疏选择虽然能够降低计算量，但使用块质心或平均相关性进行选择时，块内一个高度相关的 token 可能被大量无关 token 的低相关性掩盖，导致该块被错误忽略。论文将这一问题定义为 mean dilution，并希望在不牺牲规则块稀疏执行效率的前提下恢复这类潜在重要块。

Method: RBS-Attention 包含两个互补的块选择分支：1）centroid base 分支，利用块质心捕获平均相关性；2）rescue 分支，利用 key block 内的最大半径，并结合其随 prompt、层和注意力头变化的分布，识别可能被均值低估的块。两个分支分别进行阈值化，再合并各自的 mask，从而独立控制 rescue 块的引入比例，同时保持规则块稀疏 FlashAttention 执行。实验还考察了实际保留率、相同稀疏密度下的选择器比较、块大小、阈值和显存行为。

Result: 在 H100 GPU、Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8、128K 上下文下，RBS-Attention 实现了 20.65 倍独立预填充注意力加速、11.92 倍 vLLM 预填充注意力加速，以及 5.97 倍端到端首 token 延迟加速。在稠密 Qwen3-32B 上，其整体 RULER 准确率为 88.65，而稠密注意力为 89.52；LongBench-v2、InfiniteBench 和 Video-MME 也用于补充质量评估。结果表明，该方法在显著降低计算成本的同时，仅造成有限的质量下降。

Conclusion: 实验支持基于半径自适应的双分支块选择是长上下文预填充的一种有效方案。其核心价值在于同时保留块级平均相关性判断能力，并专门挽救可能因均值稀释而被漏选的高价值块，从而实现速度、显存效率与模型质量之间的较好平衡。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is increasingly limited by prefill, where dense self-attention processes the entire prompt before generation begins. Sparse block selection can reduce this cost, but a block centroid may hide a highly relevant token among many irrelevant ones. We call this failure mode mean dilution and propose RBS-Attention, a training-free sparse-prefill method with two complementary selection branches. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescue branch uses the maximum key-block radius and its prompt-, layer-, and head-dependent distribution to identify blocks at risk of underestimation. Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [160] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力感知路由（AAR），在冻结基础Transformer的前提下，仅利用滑动窗口注意力权重提取的时间与频谱特征训练MoE路由器。AAR在OLMoE上相较仅路由SFT基线使GSM8K提升3.37个百分点，并揭示路由与注意力之间存在跨层耦合；其效果具有明显的层深度敏感性，数学推理与事实检索之间存在深度相关的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mixture-of-Experts语言模型的路由器主要依据当前token的隐藏状态选择专家，利用的上下文信息有限，可能无法充分捕捉模型内部已经形成的全局或时序上下文状态。本文旨在探究注意力模式是否包含对路由有用且独立于隐藏状态的信息，并分析路由变化如何反过来影响后续注意力与生成行为。

Method: 提出Attention-Aware Routing（AAR）：从注意力权重的滑动窗口中提取时间特征和频谱特征，并将其用于增强MoE路由器。实验中完全冻结基础Transformer，仅训练路由参数，从而将性能变化归因于路由机制本身。进一步通过跨层分析，考察第l层路由变化经残差流传播后对第l+1层注意力汇（attention sinks）的影响，并比较不同网络深度引入AAR的效果。

Result: 在OLMoE上，AAR相较仅进行路由训练的SFT基线，在GSM8K上提升3.37个百分点。分析表明，路由与注意力构成耦合回路：第l层的路由变化能够放大第l+1层的注意力汇，并在未直接更新注意力模块的情况下重塑注意力。AAR还减少了错误答案对应的过长发散式生成，而正确答案长度基本不变。效果具有显著层深度敏感性：在所有层 indiscriminately 应用AAR可能损害事实检索，但在更深层引入时，数学推理收益仍能保持。

Conclusion: 注意力权重不仅是信息聚合机制，也携带可用于专家路由的上下文状态信息。AAR证明了冻结模型、仅优化路由即可改善推理能力，并揭示路由会通过残差流间接调节后续注意力。不同深度上存在事实检索与数学推理的权衡，因此层选择性AAR可作为研究不同层中路由相关信息的受控工具。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [161] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果潜变量修订（CaLR）框架，将推理建模为受约束的潜变量优化，以结合自回归模型的因果结构与扩散语言模型的并行生成能力。CaLR利用专家模型提供的因果拓扑矩阵，并通过隐式微分进行梯度引导的“思维修订”，动态纠正中间推理步骤、增强逻辑一致性。在复杂基准和数独等受约束任务上，方法取得当前最佳的扩散语言模型性能，超过强自回归基线，并表现出更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型虽然具备严格的因果生成结构，但容易产生局部贪婪问题；扩散语言模型支持并行生成和全局修订，却通常缺乏推理所需的严格因果结构。论文旨在融合二者优势，解决扩散式推理中的因果性与逻辑一致性不足，以及自回归推理难以回溯修正的问题。

Method: 提出Causal Latent Revision（CaLR）。首先从专家模型中提取因果拓扑矩阵（CTM），用于刻画推理步骤之间的因果依赖；随后将推理过程重新表述为受约束的潜变量优化问题，并借助隐式微分获得优化方向。在并行生成过程中，CaLR利用梯度引导对中间“思维”进行动态修订，从而在保持并行性的同时施加因果约束并纠正不一致的推理步骤。

Result: 实验表明，CaLR在复杂基准测试上达到了扩散语言模型领域的当前最佳性能，并超过了具有竞争力的自回归模型基线。在数独等具有严格约束的任务中，CaLR表现出更强的鲁棒性，说明其能够有效提升受约束推理和逻辑一致性。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或绝对提升幅度。

Conclusion: CaLR证明了通过因果拓扑约束与潜变量梯度修订，可以弥合自回归模型和扩散语言模型在推理机制上的差异。该框架使扩散语言模型能够在并行生成中进行动态自我纠错，并兼顾因果结构、逻辑一致性和生成效率，显示出用于复杂及受约束推理任务的潜力。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [162] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向水下合成孔径声呐（SAS）自动目标识别的三阶段参数高效适配框架，并通过任务级地理划分验证：冻结DINOv3 ViT主干后，仅使用LoRA适配即可显著提升识别性能，而硬负样本挖掘和监督式对比学习未带来额外显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下SAS自动目标识别面临标注目标图像稀缺、声学背景杂波严重，以及人工评估参与度较高等问题。直接使用自然图像预训练的视觉Transformer存在域差异，因此需要一种参数高效、能够适应水下声学传播特征，同时抑制岩石和沉积物等声学伪目标影响的方法。

Method: 研究采用DINOv3 Vision Transformer作为基础模型，设计三阶段参数高效框架：第一阶段冻结ViT主干，仅通过低秩适配（LoRA）完成从自然图像到水下SAS数据的域适配；第二阶段采用硬负样本挖掘，强化模型对岩石、沉积构造等声学相似物的判别边界；第三阶段使用监督式对比学习（SupCon），进一步拉开目标与杂波在表征空间中的距离。实验使用海上SAS数据，按任务级地理区域划分训练与测试集，在85%测试召回率下比较各方法，并对每项比较使用3个随机种子进行重复实验。

Result: LoRA是主要性能增益来源：在冻结相同ViT主干的条件下，AUPRC由0.300提升至0.679±0.027；秩为4时仅训练0.26%的模型参数即可达到该结果。与规模匹配的随机课程相比，硬负样本挖掘使AUPRC变化为−0.0045±0.0119；在此基础上加入SupCon，相对前一阶段变化为+0.0002±0.0096。两种增量改进均未超过对应对照，说明其收益在统计和实际意义上都很有限。

Conclusion: 对于该水下SAS ATR任务，一次有效的LoRA适配已足以获得主要性能提升，继续堆叠硬负样本挖掘和SupCon并未带来可验证的额外收益。可能原因是编码器已经拟合了挖掘到的困难样本，且监督训练阶段已基本形成目标—杂波的判别几何结构。研究结论支持优先采用简洁、低参数开销的单阶段适配方案，而非默认增加多阶段精炼流程。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [163] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究注意力图中的信息流拓扑结构，利用Forman-Ricci曲率识别与幻觉相关的信息瓶颈，并提出单次前向传播的幻觉检测方法。该方法融合注意力头的半局部与全局信息流特征，在多个大语言模型和两个基准上优于现有注意力方法及多响应基线。分析表明，因果生成中的上下文共享受损与幻觉密切相关，具体表现为过度依赖自注意力、早期令牌的上下文检索弥散，以及信息过度挤压，且在最后一层尤为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型幻觉检测方法可能依赖多次生成、外部信息或表层注意力统计，难以高效、稳定地刻画模型内部导致幻觉的信息流机制。因此，本文试图从注意力图的拓扑结构出发，识别与幻觉相关的信息瓶颈和上下文传递异常，并构建无需多次响应的单次检测方法。

Method: 首先，将模型注意力表示为注意力图，并使用Forman-Ricci曲率分析其拓扑结构，以定位潜在的信息瓶颈。随后，提取与幻觉响应相关的注意力头特征，联合建模半局部和全局信息流特征，形成单次前向传播的幻觉检测方法。实验覆盖多个大语言模型、两个幻觉检测基准，并与现有注意力方法及多响应基线进行比较；同时通过进一步分析探究不同层中的上下文共享模式。

Result: 该方法在两个幻觉检测基准上相较现有注意力型方法和多响应基线取得稳定改进，并在不同模型架构上保持具有竞争力的性能。结构分析显示，幻觉响应通常伴随上下文共享能力下降，尤其在最终Transformer层表现明显，具体包括过度依赖自注意力、从早期令牌检索上下文时信息过度弥散，以及信息过度挤压。

Conclusion: 注意力图的拓扑信息流特征能够有效区分幻觉与非幻觉响应，并支持高效的单次检测。研究进一步表明，幻觉不仅是输出层面的错误，也与生成过程中令牌间上下文传递受阻密切相关；最终层的信息瓶颈和上下文共享失衡可能是重要的内部机制。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [164] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究微调如何改变大语言模型的内部表征，以及这些变化与任务相关组件之间的关系。结果表明，EAP识别出的关键组件具有层级功能局部化特征，但其分布与表征变化最大的层并不一致；不同任务间组件重叠也不必然带来迁移，甚至可能导致性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管微调已广泛用于将大语言模型适配到下游任务，但其如何重塑模型内部机制仍缺乏清晰理解。作者希望揭示微调对注意力模式、逐层激活等内部表征的影响，并检验这些变化是否与驱动任务性能的任务相关组件相对应。

Method: 分析微调前后大语言模型的内部表征变化，包括注意力模式和层级激活；使用EAP识别与任务性能相关的组件，例如注意力头和logit层级激活；进一步比较这些组件在不同层中的分布、其与表征变化显著层之间的相关性，以及不同任务之间组件重叠与跨任务性能迁移或干扰的关系。

Result: EAP识别出的任务相关组件集中于特定层，说明任务行为的内部化具有一定功能局部化特征。然而，这些组件的层级分布与微调期间表征变化最显著的层基本不相关。此外，不同任务之间的EAP组件重叠并不必然带来跨任务性能迁移；当任务性质不同，例如分类任务与生成任务时，组件重叠尤其不能保证迁移。更具体地，对一个任务进行微调可能损害另一个任务的性能，即使两者具有较高的EAP组件重叠。

Conclusion: 微调引起的显著表征变化不一定发生在真正承担任务功能的组件所在层，因此表征变化与功能变化需要区分分析。EAP组件重叠也不能单独作为任务可迁移性的指标；任务性质和组件间潜在竞争同样重要，高重叠甚至可能预示负迁移或灾难性干扰。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [165] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种质量门控的后训练结构转换框架，用受限的CeNN启发细胞递归层替换部分注意力层，并通过表示保真度与NLL双重标准决定接受或回滚。实验表明，仅部分层可安全转换：SmolLM2-135M接受第0–2层、拒绝第3层；Qwen3.5-0.8B接受第3、7、11层。困惑度变化较小、缓存最多减少6.01%，但下游准确率仅为28.5%–32.0%，且未证明普遍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型中的注意力层承担特定表示变换，直接替换为其他模块可能破坏后续层所依赖的表示分布。因此，研究动机是探索一种保守、可验证的结构转换方法，在尽量保持模型行为的同时减少缓存开销，并避免未经验证的全局注意力替换。

Method: 提出quality-gated post-training conversion框架，采用CeNN启发的细胞递归层，包含有界局部处理、紧凑递归记忆、路由与融合机制，以及accept-or-rollback验证流程。研究比较Integrated Memory、MemoryFusion和PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32三种实现。严格的PDelta3转换同时检查表示保真度和负对数似然（NLL）阈值；只有通过固定阈值的层才被接受，否则回滚。

Result: 在SmolLM2-135M上，第0–2层被接受，累计ΔNLL为+0.01209；第3层虽然NLL可接受，但因表示保真度不达标而被拒绝。在Qwen3.5-0.8B上，第3、7、11层被接受，最终ΔNLL为+0.02073。Integrated Memory使困惑度变化保持在−0.07%至+0.93%，总缓存最多减少6.01%。对转换后的Qwen版本进行的200题抽样下游检查显示总体准确率为28.5%–32.0%。
